```
1→"""CLI entry point: parse args, load shared context, dispatch command handlers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→import sys
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.cli_support.parser import create_parser as _create_parser
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import LangResolutionError, resolve_lang
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import CommandRuntime
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.registry import get_command_handlers
    13→from desloppify.base.config import load_config
    14→from desloppify.base.discovery.source import set_exclusions
    15→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import CommandError
    16→from desloppify.base.output.fallbacks import log_best_effort_failure
    17→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    18→from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_default_path, get_project_root
    19→from desloppify.base.registry import detector_names, on_detector_registered
    20→from desloppify.base.runtime_state import runtime_scope
    21→from desloppify.languages import available_langs
    22→from desloppify.state import load_state
    23→
    24→_DETECTOR_NAMES_CACHE: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    25→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    26→
    27→
    28→def _get_detector_names() -> list[str]:
    29→    """Return cached detector names, computing on first access."""
    30→    if "names" not in _DETECTOR_NAMES_CACHE:
    31→        _DETECTOR_NAMES_CACHE["names"] = detector_names()
    32→    return _DETECTOR_NAMES_CACHE["names"]
    33→
    34→
    35→def _invalidate_detector_names_cache() -> None:
    36→    """Invalidate detector-name cache when runtime registrations change."""
    37→    _DETECTOR_NAMES_CACHE.pop("names", None)
    38→
    39→
    40→on_detector_registered(_invalidate_detector_names_cache)
    41→
    42→
    43→def create_parser():
    44→    """Return the top-level argparse parser."""
    45→    return _create_parser(langs=available_langs(), detector_names=_get_detector_names())
    46→
    47→
    48→def _apply_persisted_exclusions(args, config: dict):
    49→    """Merge CLI --exclude with persisted config.exclude and apply globally."""
    50→    cli_exclusions = getattr(args, "exclude", None) or []
    51→    persisted = config.get("exclude", [])
    52→    combined = list(cli_exclusions) + [e for e in persisted if e not in cli_exclusions]
    53→    if not combined:
    54→        return
    55→    set_exclusions(combined)
    56→    if cli_exclusions:
    57→        print(
    58→            colorize(f"  Excluding: {', '.join(combined)}", "dim"),
    59→            file=sys.stderr,
    60→        )
    61→        return
    62→    print(
    63→        colorize(
    64→            f"  Excluding (from config): {', '.join(combined)}", "dim"
    65→        ),
    66→        file=sys.stderr,
    67→    )
    68→
    69→
    70→def _resolve_default_path(args) -> None:
    71→    """Fill args.path from detected language or default source path.
    72→
    73→    For the review command, the last scan path (stored in state) is used as the
    74→    default so that ``desloppify review --prepare`` works on the same scope as
    75→    the preceding scan even when the project files are not under ``src/``.
    76→    """
    77→    if getattr(args, "path", None) is not None:
    78→        return
    79→    runtime_root = get_project_root()
    80→    if getattr(args, "command", None) == "review":
    81→        try:
    82→            state_file = state_path(args)
    83→            if state_file:
    84→                saved = load_state(state_file)
    85→                saved_path = saved.get("scan_path")
    86→                if saved_path:
    87→                    args.path = str((runtime_root / saved_path).resolve())
    88→                    return
    89→        except (OSError, KeyError, ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError) as exc:
    90→            log_best_effort_failure(logger, "resolve default review path from saved state", exc)
    91→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
    92→    if lang:
    93→        args.path = str(runtime_root / lang.default_src)
    94→    else:
    95→        args.path = str(get_default_path())
    96→
    97→
    98→def _load_shared_runtime(args) -> None:
    99→    """Load config/state and attach shared objects to parsed args."""
   100→    config = load_config()
   101→
   102→    state_file = state_path(args)
   103→    state = load_state(state_file)
   104→    _apply_persisted_exclusions(args, config)
   105→
   106→    args.runtime = CommandRuntime(config=config, state=state, state_path=state_file)
   107→
   108→
   109→def _resolve_handler(command: str):
   110→    return get_command_handlers()[command]
   111→
   112→
   113→def _handle_help_command(args, parser) -> None:
   114→    """Handle explicit help command when present in parser config."""
   115→    topic = list(getattr(args, "topic", []) or [])
   116→    try:
   117→        parser.parse_args([*topic, "--help"])
   118→    except SystemExit:
   119→        return
   120→
   121→
   122→def main() -> None:
   123→    # Ensure Unicode output works on Windows terminals (cp1252 etc.)
   124→    for stream in (sys.stdout, sys.stderr):
   125→        if hasattr(stream, "reconfigure"):
   126→            try:
   127→                stream.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
   128→            except (AttributeError, OSError):
   129→                logger.debug(
   130→                    "Skipping stream reconfigure for %s (not supported)",
   131→                    getattr(stream, "name", "<stream>"),
   132→                )
   133→
   134→    parser = create_parser()
   135→    args = parser.parse_args()
   136→    if args.command == "help":
   137→        _handle_help_command(args, parser)
   138→        return
   139→
   140→    try:
   141→        with runtime_scope():
   142→            _resolve_default_path(args)
   143→            _load_shared_runtime(args)
   144→
   145→            handler = _resolve_handler(args.command)
   146→            handler(args)
   147→    except CommandError as exc:
   148→        print(colorize(f"  {exc.message}", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   149→        sys.exit(exc.exit_code)
   150→    except LangResolutionError as exc:
   151→        print(colorize(f"  {exc.message}", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   152→        sys.exit(1)
   153→    except KeyboardInterrupt:
   154→        print("\nInterrupted.")
   155→        sys.exit(1)
   156→
   157→
   158→if __name__ == "__main__":
   159→    main()
   160→
```

```
    43→def cmd_plan_output(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    44→    """Generate a prioritized markdown plan from state."""
    45→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    46→    state = runtime.state
    47→
    48→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    49→        return
    50→
    51→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(runtime.config)
    52→    if config_warning:
    53→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    54→
    55→    plan_md = planning_mod.generate_plan_md(state)
    56→    next_command = "desloppify next --count 20"
    57→
    58→    output = getattr(args, "output", None)
    59→    if output:
    60→        try:
    61→            safe_write_text(output, plan_md)
    62→            print(colorize(f"Plan written to {output}", "green"))
    63→            print_agent_plan(
    64→                ["Inspect and execute the generated plan."],
    65→                next_command=next_command,
    66→            )
    67→        except OSError as e:
    68→            warn_best_effort(f"Could not write plan to {output}: {e}")
    69→    else:
    70→        print(plan_md)
    71→        print()
    72→        print_agent_plan(
    73→            ["Start from the top-ranked action in this plan."],
    74→            next_command=next_command,
    75→        )
    76→
    77→
    78→def _cmd_plan_generate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    79→    """Generate the prioritized markdown plan (existing behavior)."""
    80→    cmd_plan_output(args)
    81→
    82→
    83→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    84→    """Show plan metadata summary."""
    85→    plan = load_plan()
    86→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    87→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    88→    total_skipped = len(skipped)
    89→    kind_counts = {
    90→        kind: sum(1 for entry in skipped.values() if entry.get("kind") == kind)
    91→        for kind in USER_SKIP_KINDS
    92→    }
    93→    temp_count = kind_counts["temporary"]
    94→    perm_count = kind_counts["permanent"]
    95→    fp_count = kind_counts["false_positive"]
    96→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    97→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    98→    superseded = len(plan.get("superseded", {}))
    99→
   100→    described, noted = annotation_counts(plan)
   101→
   102→    print(colorize("  Living Plan Status", "bold"))
   103→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 40, "dim"))
   104→    print(f"  Queue:            {ordered} items prioritized")
   105→    if total_skipped:
   106→        print(f"  Skipped:          {total_skipped} (temp: {temp_count}, wontfix: {perm_count}, fp: {fp_count})")
   107→    else:
   108→        print("  Skipped:          0")
   109→    print(f"  Clusters:         {len(clusters)}")
   110→    if clusters:
   111→        for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   112→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   113→            member_count = len(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   114→            marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
   115→            desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
   116→            print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{desc_str}{marker}")
   117→    if described or noted:
   118→        print(f"  Annotations:      {described} described, {noted} noted")
   119→    if active:
   120→        print(f"  Focus:            {active}")
   121→    if superseded:
   122→        print(f"  Disappeared:      {superseded} (resolved or removed since last scan)")
   123→
   124→    # Commit tracking summary
   125→    _cfg = load_config()
   126→    if _cfg.get("commit_tracking_enabled", True):
   127→        ct = commit_tracking_summary(plan)
   128→        if ct["total"] > 0:
   129→            pr_num = _cfg.get("commit_pr", 0)
   130→            pr_str = f"  PR: #{pr_num}" if pr_num else ""
   131→            print(
   132→                f"  Commit tracking:  {ct['uncommitted']} uncommitted, "
   133→                f"{ct['committed']} committed ({ct['total']} issues){pr_str}"
   134→            )
   135→
   136→
   137→def _cmd_plan_reset(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   138→    """Reset the plan to empty."""
   139→    plan = load_plan()
   140→    queue_len = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   141→    cluster_count = len(plan.get("clusters", {}))
   142→    reset_plan(plan)
   143→    append_log_entry(
   144→        plan, "reset", actor="user",
   145→        detail={"previous_queue_size": queue_len, "previous_cluster_count": cluster_count},
   146→    )
   147→    save_plan(plan)
   148→    print(colorize("  Plan reset to empty.", "green"))
   149→
   150→
   151→_PLAN_ACTION_HANDLERS = {
   152→    "show": _cmd_plan_show,
   153→    "queue": cmd_plan_queue,
   154→    "reset": _cmd_plan_reset,
   155→    "reorder": cmd_plan_reorder,
   156→    "describe": cmd_plan_describe,
   157→    "resolve": cmd_plan_resolve,
   158→    "note": cmd_plan_note,
   159→    "focus": cmd_plan_focus,
   160→    "skip": cmd_plan_skip,
   161→    "unskip": cmd_plan_unskip,
   162→    "reopen": cmd_plan_reopen,
   163→    "cluster": cmd_cluster_dispatch,
   164→    "triage": cmd_plan_triage,
   165→    "commit-log": cmd_commit_log_dispatch,
   166→}
   167→
   168→
   169→def cmd_plan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   170→    """Dispatch plan subcommand or generate markdown output."""
   171→    plan_action = getattr(args, "plan_action", None)
   172→    if plan_action is None:
   173→        _cmd_plan_generate(args)
   174→        return
   175→
   176→    handler = _PLAN_ACTION_HANDLERS.get(plan_action)
   177→    if handler is None:
   178→        print(f"Unknown plan action: {plan_action}")
   179→        return
   180→    handler(args)
   181→
   182→__all__ = ["cmd_plan", "cmd_plan_output"]
   183→
```

```
    47→    """All user-facing options for the ``next`` command, extracted once."""
    48→
    49→    count: int = 1
    50→    scope: str | None = None
    51→    status: str = "open"
    52→    group: str = "item"
    53→    explain: bool = False
    54→    cluster: str | None = None
    55→    include_skipped: bool = False
    56→    output_file: str | None = None
    57→    output_format: str = "terminal"
    58→
    59→    @classmethod
    60→    def from_args(cls, args: argparse.Namespace) -> NextOptions:
    61→        """Build from an argparse Namespace, applying defaults for missing attrs."""
    62→        return cls(
    63→            count=getattr(args, "count", 1) or 1,
    64→            scope=getattr(args, "scope", None),
    65→            status=getattr(args, "status", "open"),
    66→            group=getattr(args, "group", "item"),
    67→            explain=bool(getattr(args, "explain", False)),
    68→            cluster=getattr(args, "cluster", None),
    69→            include_skipped=bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False)),
    70→            output_file=getattr(args, "output", None),
    71→            output_format=getattr(args, "format", "terminal"),
    72→        )
    73→
    74→
    75→def _scorecard_subjective(
    76→    state: dict,
    77→    dim_scores: dict,
    78→) -> list[dict]:
    79→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""
    80→    return _scorecard_subjective_impl(state, dim_scores)
    81→
    82→
    83→def _low_subjective_dimensions(
    84→    state: dict,
    85→    dim_scores: dict,
    86→    *,
    87→    threshold: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    88→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:
    89→    """Return assessed scorecard-subjective entries below the threshold."""
    90→    low: list[tuple[str, float, int]] = []
    91→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):
    92→        if entry.get("placeholder"):
    93→            continue
    94→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    95→        if strict_val < threshold:
    96→            low.append(
    97→                (
    98→                    str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")),
    99→                    strict_val,
   100→                    int(entry.get("failing", 0)),
   101→                )
   102→            )
   103→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
   104→    return low
   105→
   106→
   107→def cmd_next(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   108→    """Show next highest-priority queue items."""
   109→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   110→    state = runtime.state
   111→    config = runtime.config
   112→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   113→        return
   114→
   115→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
   116→    if skill_warning:
   117→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
   118→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
   119→    if config_warning:
   120→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
   121→
   122→    print_triage_guardrail_info(state=state)
   123→    _get_items(args, state, config)
   124→
   125→
   126→def _resolve_cluster_focus(
   127→    plan_data: dict | None,
   128→    *,
   129→    cluster_arg: str | None,
   130→    scope: str | None,
   131→) -> str | None:
   132→    effective_cluster = cluster_arg
   133→    if plan_data and not cluster_arg and not scope:
   134→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
   135→        if active_cluster:
   136→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
   137→    return effective_cluster
   138→
   139→
   140→def _build_next_payload(
   141→    *,
   142→    queue: dict,
   143→    items: list[dict],
   144→    state: dict,
   145→    narrative: dict,
   146→    plan_data: dict | None,
   147→) -> dict:
   148→    payload = next_output_mod.build_query_payload(
   149→        queue, items, command="next", narrative=narrative, plan=plan_data
   150→    )
   151→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   152→    payload["overall_score"] = scores.overall
   153→    payload["objective_score"] = scores.objective
   154→    payload["strict_score"] = scores.strict
   155→    payload["scorecard_dimensions"] = scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   156→        state,
   157→        dim_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   158→    )
   159→    payload["subjective_measures"] = [
   160→        row for row in payload["scorecard_dimensions"] if row.get("subjective")
   161→    ]
   162→    return payload
   163→
   164→
   165→def _emit_requested_output(
   166→    opts: NextOptions,
   167→    payload: dict,
   168→    items: list[dict],
   169→) -> bool:
   170→    if opts.output_file:
   171→        if next_output_mod.write_output_file(
   172→            opts.output_file,
   173→            payload,
   174→            len(items),
   175→            safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   176→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   177→        ):
   178→            return True
   179→        raise SystemExit(1)
   180→
   181→    if next_output_mod.emit_non_terminal_output(opts.output_format, payload, items):
   182→        return True
   183→    return False
   184→
   185→
   186→def _plan_queue_context(
   187→    *,
   188→    state: dict,
   189→    plan_data: dict | None,
   190→    context=None,
   191→) -> tuple[float | None, QueueBreakdown | None]:
   192→    effective_plan = context.plan if context is not None else plan_data
   193→    plan_start_strict = get_plan_start_strict(effective_plan)
   194→    try:
   195→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan_data, context=context)
   196→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   197→        breakdown = None
   198→    return plan_start_strict, breakdown
   199→
   200→
   201→def _merge_potentials_safe(raw_potentials: dict | None) -> dict | None:
   202→    try:
   203→        return merge_potentials(raw_potentials) or None
   204→    except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError):
   205→        return raw_potentials or None
   206→
   207→
   208→def _get_items(args: argparse.Namespace, state: dict, config: dict) -> None:
   209→    opts = NextOptions.from_args(args)
   210→
   211→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config)
   212→
   213→    # Load the living plan
   214→    plan = load_plan()
   215→    plan_data: dict | None = None
   216→    if (
   217→        plan.get("queue_order")
   218→        or plan.get("overrides")
   219→        or plan.get("clusters")
   220→    ):
   221→        plan_data = plan
   222→
   223→    # Build unified context once — all downstream consumers agree on
   224→    # plan, target_strict, and subjective visibility policy.
   225→    ctx = queue_context(
   226→        state, config=config, plan=plan_data, target_strict=target_strict,
   227→    )
   228→
   229→    # Auto-scope to focus cluster if set and no explicit scope/cluster
   230→    effective_cluster = _resolve_cluster_focus(
   231→        plan_data,
   232→        cluster_arg=opts.cluster,
   233→        scope=opts.scope,
   234→    )
   235→
   236→    queue = build_work_queue(
   237→        state,
   238→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   239→            count=opts.count,
   240→            scope=opts.scope,
   241→            status=opts.status,
   242→            include_subjective=True,
   243→            subjective_threshold=target_strict,
   244→            explain=opts.explain,
   245→            include_skipped=opts.include_skipped,
   246→            cluster=effective_cluster,
   247→            context=ctx,
   248→        ),
   249→    )
   250→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   251→
   252→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   253→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   254→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   255→        state,
   256→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="next", plan=plan_data),
   257→    )
   258→
   259→    payload = _build_next_payload(
   260→        queue=queue,
   261→        items=items,
   262→        state=state,
   263→        narrative=narrative,
   264→        plan_data=plan_data,
   265→    )
   266→    write_query(payload)
   267→
   268→    if _emit_requested_output(opts, payload, items):
   269→        return
   270→
   271→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   272→    issues_scoped = state_mod.path_scoped_issues(
   273→        state.get("issues", {}),
   274→        state.get("scan_path"),
   275→    )
   276→
   277→    # Extract frozen plan-start score and queue breakdown for lifecycle display
   278→    plan_start_strict, breakdown = _plan_queue_context(
   279→        state=state,
   280→        plan_data=plan_data,
   281→        context=ctx,
   282→    )
   283→    queue_total = breakdown.queue_total if breakdown else 0
   284→
   285→    _render_queue_header(queue, opts.explain)
   286→    strict_score = state_mod.score_snapshot(state).strict
   287→    if _show_empty_queue(
   288→        queue,
   289→        strict_score,
   290→        plan_start_strict=plan_start_strict,
   291→        target_strict=target_strict,
   292→    ):
   293→        return
   294→
   295→    raw_potentials = state.get("potentials", {})
   296→    potentials = _merge_potentials_safe(raw_potentials)
   297→    next_render_mod.render_terminal_items(
   298→        items, dim_scores, issues_scoped, group=opts.group, explain=opts.explain,
   299→        potentials=potentials, plan=plan_data,
   300→        cluster_filter=effective_cluster,
   301→    )
   302→    next_nudges_mod.render_single_item_resolution_hint(items)
   303→    next_nudges_mod.render_uncommitted_reminder(plan_data)
   304→    next_nudges_mod.render_followup_nudges(
   305→        state,
   306→        dim_scores,
   307→        issues_scoped,
   308→        strict_score=strict_score,
   309→        target_strict_score=target_strict,
   310→        queue_total=queue_total,
   311→        plan_start_strict=plan_start_strict,
   312→        breakdown=breakdown,
   313→    )
   314→    print()
   315→
   316→
   317→__all__ = ["NextOptions", "_low_subjective_dimensions", "cmd_next"]
   318→
```

```
    55→def _resolve_state_file(path: Path | None) -> Path:

    59→def _resolve_plan_file(path: Path | None) -> Path:

    63→def _plan_file_for_state(state_file: Path | None) -> Path | None:
    64→    if state_file is None:
    65→        return None
    66→    return plan_path_for_state(state_file)
    67→
    68→
    69→def _snapshot_file(path: Path) -> str | None:
    70→    if not path.exists():
    71→        return None
    72→    return path.read_text()
    73→
    74→
    75→def _restore_file_snapshot(path: Path, snapshot: str | None) -> None:
    76→    if snapshot is None:
    77→        try:
    78→            path.unlink()
    79→        except FileNotFoundError:
    80→            return
    81→        return
    82→    safe_write_text(path, snapshot)

    84→
    85→def _save_plan_state_transactional(
    86→    *,
    87→    plan: dict,
    88→    plan_path: Path | None,
    89→    state_data: dict,
    90→    state_path_value: Path | None,
    91→) -> None:
    92→    """Persist plan+state together; rollback both files on partial write failure."""
    93→    effective_plan_path = _resolve_plan_file(plan_path)
    94→    effective_state_path = _resolve_state_file(state_path_value)
    95→    plan_snapshot = _snapshot_file(effective_plan_path)
    96→    state_snapshot = _snapshot_file(effective_state_path)
    97→
    98→    try:
    99→        state_mod.save_state(state_data, effective_state_path)
   100→        save_plan(plan, effective_plan_path)
   101→    except Exception:
   102→        _restore_file_snapshot(effective_state_path, state_snapshot)
   103→        _restore_file_snapshot(effective_plan_path, plan_snapshot)
   104→        raise

   107→def cmd_plan_describe(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:

   110→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   111→        return
   112→
   113→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   114→    text: str = getattr(args, "text", "")
   115→
   116→    plan = load_plan()
   117→    issue_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
   118→    if not issue_ids:
   119→        print(colorize("  No matching issues found.", "yellow"))
   120→        return
   121→
   122→    for fid in issue_ids:
   123→        describe_issue(plan, fid, text or None)
   124→    append_log_entry(
   125→        plan, "describe", issue_ids=issue_ids, actor="user",
   126→        detail={"text": text or None},
   127→    )
   128→    save_plan(plan)
   129→    print(colorize(f"  Set description on {len(issue_ids)} issue(s).", "green"))
   130→

   162→def _validate_skip_requirements(
   163→    *,
   164→    kind: str,
   165→    attestation: str | None,
   166→    note: str | None,
   167→) -> bool:
   168→    if not skip_kind_requires_attestation(kind):
   169→        return True
   170→    if not validate_attestation(attestation):
   171→        show_attestation_requirement(
   172→            "Permanent skip" if kind == "permanent" else "False positive",
   173→            attestation,
   174→            ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
   175→        )
   176→        return False
   177→    if skip_kind_requires_note(kind) and not note:
   178→        print(
   179→            colorize("  --permanent requires --note to explain the decision.", "yellow"),
   180→            file=sys.stderr,
   181→        )
   182→        return False
   183→    return True
   184→

   186→def _apply_state_skip_resolution(
   187→    *,
   188→    kind: str,
   189→    state_file: Path | None,
   190→    issue_ids: list[str],
   191→    note: str | None,
   192→    attestation: str | None,
   193→) -> dict | None:
   194→    status = skip_kind_state_status(kind)
   195→    if status is None:
   196→        return None
   197→    state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   198→    for fid in issue_ids:
   199→        state_mod.resolve_issues(
   200→            state_data,
   201→            fid,
   202→            status,
   203→            note or "",
   204→            attestation=attestation,
   205→        )
   206→    return state_data

   209→def cmd_plan_skip(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   210→    """Skip issues — unified command for temporary/permanent/false-positive."""
   211→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   212→    state = runtime.state
   213→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   214→        return
   215→
   216→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   217→    reason: str | None = getattr(args, "reason", None)
   218→    review_after: int | None = getattr(args, "review_after", None)
   219→    permanent: bool = getattr(args, "permanent", False)
   220→    false_positive: bool = getattr(args, "false_positive", False)
   221→    note: str | None = getattr(args, "note", None)
   222→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attest", None)
   223→
   224→    kind = skip_kind_from_flags(permanent=permanent, false_positive=false_positive)
   225→    if not _validate_skip_requirements(
   226→        kind=kind,
   227→        attestation=attestation,
   228→        note=note,
   229→    ):
   230→        return
   231→
   232→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   233→    plan_file = _plan_file_for_state(state_file)
   234→    plan = load_plan(plan_file)
   235→    issue_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
   236→    if not issue_ids:
   237→        print(colorize("  No matching issues found.", "yellow"))
   238→        return
   239→
   240→    # For permanent/false_positive: delegate to state layer for score impact
   241→    state_data = _apply_state_skip_resolution(
   242→        kind=kind,
   243→        state_file=state_file,
   244→        issue_ids=issue_ids,
   245→        note=note,
   246→        attestation=attestation,
   247→    )
   248→
   249→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   250→    count = skip_items(
   251→        plan,
   252→        issue_ids,
   253→        kind=kind,
   254→        reason=reason,
   255→        note=note,
   256→        attestation=attestation,
   257→        review_after=review_after,
   258→        scan_count=scan_count,
   259→    )
   260→
   261→    # Log the skip action
   262→    append_log_entry(
   263→        plan,
   264→        "skip",
   265→        issue_ids=issue_ids,
   266→        actor="user",
   267→        note=note,
   268→        detail={"kind": kind, "reason": reason},
   269→    )
   270→    if state_data is not None:
   271→        _save_plan_state_transactional(
   272→            plan=plan,
   273→            plan_path=plan_file,
   274→            state_data=state_data,
   275→            state_path_value=state_file,
   276→        )
   277→    else:
   278→        save_plan(plan, plan_file)
   279→
   280→    print(colorize(f"  {SKIP_KIND_LABELS[kind]} {count} item(s).", "green"))
   281→    if review_after:
   282→        print(colorize(f"  Will re-surface after {review_after} scan(s).", "dim"))
   283→
   284→
   285→def cmd_plan_unskip(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   286→    """Unskip issues — bring back to queue."""
   287→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   288→    state = runtime.state
   289→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   290→        return
   291→
   292→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   293→
   294→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   295→    plan_file = _plan_file_for_state(state_file)
   296→    plan = load_plan(plan_file)
   297→    # For unskip we need to match against all statuses (skipped items may be wontfix/fp)
   298→    issue_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan, status_filter="all")
   299→    if not issue_ids:
   300→        print(colorize("  No matching issues found.", "yellow"))
   301→        return
   302→
   303→    count, need_reopen = unskip_items(plan, issue_ids)
   304→    append_log_entry(
   305→        plan, "unskip", issue_ids=issue_ids, actor="user",
   306→        detail={"need_reopen": need_reopen},
   307→    )
   308→
   309→    # Reopen permanent/false_positive items in state
   310→    reopened: list[str] = []
   311→    if need_reopen:
   312→        state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   313→        for fid in need_reopen:
   314→            reopened.extend(state_mod.resolve_issues(state_data, fid, "open"))
   315→        _save_plan_state_transactional(
   316→            plan=plan,
   317→            plan_path=plan_file,
   318→            state_data=state_data,
   319→            state_path_value=state_file,
   320→        )
   321→        print(colorize(f"  Reopened {len(reopened)} issue(s) in state.", "dim"))
   322→    else:
   323→        save_plan(plan, plan_file)
   324→
   325→    print(colorize(f"  Unskipped {count} item(s) — back in queue.", "green"))
   326→

   330→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   331→
   332→def cmd_plan_reopen(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   333→    """Reopen resolved issues from plan context."""
   334→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   335→
   336→    raw_state_path = state_path(args)
   337→    state_file = raw_state_path if isinstance(raw_state_path, Path) else Path(raw_state_path) if raw_state_path else None
   338→    state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   339→    plan_file = _plan_file_for_state(state_file)
   340→
   341→    reopened: list[str] = []
   342→    for pattern in patterns:
   343→        reopened.extend(
   344→            state_mod.resolve_issues(state_data, pattern, "open")
   345→        )
   346→
   347→    if not reopened:
   348→        print(colorize("  No resolved issues matching: " + " ".join(patterns), "yellow"))
   349→        return
   350→
   351→    # Remove from skipped if present, and ensure all reopened IDs are in queue
   352→    plan = load_plan(plan_file)
   353→
   354→    # Remove from commit tracking uncommitted list
   355→    purge_uncommitted_ids(plan, reopened)
   356→
   357→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   358→    count = 0
   359→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   360→    for fid in reopened:
   361→        if fid in skipped:
   362→            skipped.pop(fid)
   363→            count += 1
   364→        if fid not in order:
   365→            plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   366→            order.add(fid)
   367→            count += 1
   368→    append_log_entry(
   369→        plan, "reopen", issue_ids=reopened, actor="user",
   370→    )
   371→    _save_plan_state_transactional(
   372→        plan=plan,
   373→        plan_path=plan_file,
   374→        state_data=state_data,
   375→        state_path_value=state_file,
   376→    )
   377→
   378→    print(colorize(f"  Reopened {len(reopened)} issue(s).", "green"))
   379→    if count:
   380→        print(colorize("  Plan updated: items moved back to queue.", "dim"))
   381→
   382→
   383→_CLUSTER_INDIVIDUAL_THRESHOLD = 10
   384→
   385→
   386→def _check_cluster_guard(patterns: list[str], plan: dict, state: dict) -> bool:
   387→    """Return True if blocked by cluster guard, False if OK to proceed."""
   388→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   389→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   390→    for pattern in patterns:
   391→        if pattern in clusters:
   392→            cluster = clusters[pattern]
   393→            # Filter to alive issues only — stale IDs should not count
   394→            ids = [
   395→                fid for fid in cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   396→                if fid in issues and issues[fid].get("status") == "open"
   397→            ]
   398→            if len(ids) == 0:
   399→                print(colorize(
   400→                    f"\n  Cluster '{pattern}' is empty — add items before marking it done.\n",
   401→                    "yellow",
   402→                ))
   403→                print(colorize(
   404→                    f"  Use: desloppify plan cluster add {pattern} <issue-id>",
   405→                    "dim",
   406→                ))
   407→                return True  # blocked
   408→            if len(ids) <= _CLUSTER_INDIVIDUAL_THRESHOLD:
   409→                _print_cluster_guard(pattern, ids, state)
   410→                return True  # blocked
   411→    return False  # OK
   412→
   413→
   414→def _print_cluster_guard(cluster_name: str, issue_ids: list[str], state: dict) -> None:
   415→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   416→    print(colorize(
   417→        f"\n  Cluster '{cluster_name}' has {len(issue_ids)} item(s) — mark them done individually first:\n",
   418→        "yellow",
   419→    ))
   420→    for fid in issue_ids:
   421→        f = issues.get(fid, {})
   422→        summary = f.get("summary", "(no summary)")[:80]
   423→        detector = f.get("detector", "?")
   424→        print(f"    {fid}  [{detector}]  {summary}")
   425→    print(colorize(
   426→        "\n  Use: desloppify resolve <id> --status fixed --note '...' --attest '...'",
   427→        "dim",
   428→    ))
   429→    print(colorize(
   430→        "  Or mark each resolved: desloppify plan resolve <id> --note '...' --confirm\n",
   431→        "dim",
   432→    ))
   433→
   434→
   435→def _is_synthetic_id(fid: str) -> bool:
   436→    """Return True if the ID is a synthetic workflow/triage item, not a real issue."""
   437→    return fid.startswith("triage::") or fid.startswith("workflow::") or fid.startswith("subjective::")
   438→
   439→
   440→def _resolve_synthetic_ids(patterns: list[str]) -> tuple[list[str], list[str]]:
   441→    """Separate synthetic IDs from real issue patterns.
   442→
   443→    Returns (synthetic_ids, remaining_patterns).
   444→    """
   445→    synthetic = [p for p in patterns if _is_synthetic_id(p)]
   446→    remaining = [p for p in patterns if not _is_synthetic_id(p)]
   447→    return synthetic, remaining
   448→
   449→
   450→def _blocked_triage_stages(plan: dict) -> dict[str, list[str]]:
   451→    """Return ``{stage_id: [blocked_by_ids]}`` for triage stages that can't run yet.
   452→
   453→    Uses the dependency graph and confirmed-stage metadata directly —
   454→    no state needed, no queue item construction.
   455→    """
   456→    order_set = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   457→    present = order_set & TRIAGE_IDS
   458→    if not present:
   459→        return {}
   460→
   461→    confirmed = set(plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {}).get("triage_stages", {}).keys())
   462→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit")
   463→
   464→    blocked: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
   465→    for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names, strict=False):
   466→        if sid not in present or name in confirmed:
   467→            continue
   468→        deps = TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES.get(name, set())
   469→        unmet = sorted(
   470→            f"triage::{dep}" for dep in deps
   471→            if f"triage::{dep}" in present and dep not in confirmed
   472→        )
   473→        if unmet:
   474→            blocked[sid] = unmet
   475→    return blocked
   476→
   477→
   478→def cmd_plan_resolve(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   479→    """Mark issues as fixed — delegates to cmd_resolve for rich UX."""
   480→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   481→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attest", None)
   482→    note: str | None = getattr(args, "note", None)
   483→
   484→    # --confirm: auto-generate attestation from --note
   485→    if getattr(args, "confirm", False):
   486→        if not note:
   487→            print(colorize("  --confirm requires --note to describe what you did.", "red"))
   488→            return
   489→        attestation = f"I have actually {note} and I am not gaming the score."
   490→        args.attest = attestation
   491→
   492→    # Handle synthetic IDs (triage::*, workflow::*, subjective::*) directly
   493→    synthetic_ids, real_patterns = _resolve_synthetic_ids(patterns)
   494→    if synthetic_ids:
   495→        plan = load_plan()
   496→        # Validate triage dependency chain
   497→        blocked_map = _blocked_triage_stages(plan)
   498→        for sid in synthetic_ids:
   499→            if sid in blocked_map:
   500→                deps = ", ".join(b.replace("triage::", "") for b in blocked_map[sid])
   501→                print(colorize(f"  Cannot resolve {sid} — blocked by: {deps}", "red"))
   502→                print(colorize("  Complete those stages first, or use --force-resolve to override.", "dim"))
   503→                if not getattr(args, "force_resolve", False):
   504→                    return
   505→        purge_ids(plan, synthetic_ids)
   506→        append_log_entry(
   507→            plan, "done", issue_ids=synthetic_ids, actor="user", note=note,
   508→        )
   509→        save_plan(plan)
   510→        for sid in synthetic_ids:
   511→            print(colorize(f"  Resolved: {sid}", "green"))
   512→        if not real_patterns:
   513→            return
   514→        # Continue with remaining real patterns
   515→        patterns = real_patterns
   516→        args.patterns = patterns
   517→
   518→    # Validate note length
   519→    if not validate_note_length(note):
   520→        show_note_length_requirement(note)
   521→        return
   522→
   523→    # Pre-validate attestation before delegating (avoids stale hint in resolve)
   524→    if not validate_attestation(attestation):
   525→        show_attestation_requirement("Plan resolve", attestation, ATTEST_EXAMPLE)
   526→        return
   527→
   528→    # Cluster completion guard: block bulk-completing small clusters
   529→    try:
   530→        runtime = command_runtime(args)
   531→        state = runtime.state
   532→        plan = load_plan()
   533→        if _check_cluster_guard(patterns, plan, state):
   534→            return
   535→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   536→        plan = None
   537→
   538→    # Log the done action (best-effort)
   539→    try:
   540→        if plan is None:
   541→            plan = load_plan()
   542→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   543→        cluster_name = None
   544→        for p in patterns:
   545→            if p in clusters:
   546→                cluster_name = p
   547→                break
   548→        append_log_entry(
   549→            plan,
   550→            "done",
   551→            issue_ids=patterns,
   552→            cluster_name=cluster_name,
   553→            actor="user",
   554→            note=note,
   555→        )
   556→        save_plan(plan)
   557→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   558→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "append plan resolve log entry", exc)
   559→        print(colorize(f"  Note: unable to append plan resolve log entry ({exc}).", "dim"))
   560→
   561→    # Build a Namespace that cmd_resolve expects
   562→    resolve_args = argparse.Namespace(
   563→        status="fixed",
   564→        patterns=patterns,
   565→        note=note,
   566→        attest=attestation,
   567→        confirm_batch_wontfix=False,
   568→        force_resolve=bool(getattr(args, "force_resolve", False)),
   569→        state=getattr(args, "state", None),
   570→        lang=getattr(args, "lang", None),
   571→        path=getattr(args, "path", None),
   572→        exclude=getattr(args, "exclude", None),
   573→    )
   574→
   575→    cmd_resolve(resolve_args)
   576→
   577→
   578→def cmd_plan_focus(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   579→    """Set or clear the active cluster focus."""
   580→    clear_flag = getattr(args, "clear", False)
   581→    cluster_name: str | None = getattr(args, "cluster_name", None)
   582→
   583→    plan = load_plan()
   584→    if clear_flag:
   585→        prev = plan.get("active_cluster")
   586→        clear_focus(plan)
   587→        append_log_entry(
   588→            plan, "focus", actor="user",
   589→            detail={"action": "clear", "previous": prev},
   590→        )
   591→        save_plan(plan)
   592→        print(colorize("  Focus cleared.", "green"))
   593→        return
   594→
   595→    if not cluster_name:
   596→        active = plan.get("active_cluster")
   597→        if active:
   598→            print(f"  Focused on: {active}")
   599→        else:
   600→            print("  No active focus.")
   601→        return
   602→
   603→    try:
   604→        set_focus(plan, cluster_name)
   605→    except ValueError as ex:
   606→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
   607→        return
   608→    append_log_entry(
   609→        plan, "focus", cluster_name=cluster_name, actor="user",
   610→        detail={"action": "set"},
   611→    )
   612→    save_plan(plan)
   613→    print(colorize(f"  Focused on: {cluster_name}", "green"))
   614→
   615→
   616→__all__ = [
   617→    "cmd_plan_describe",
   618→    "cmd_plan_resolve",
   619→    "cmd_plan_focus",
   620→    "cmd_plan_note",
   621→    "cmd_plan_reopen",
   622→    "cmd_plan_skip",
   623→    "cmd_plan_unskip",
   624→]
   625→
```

```
    51→def _validate_fixed_note(args: argparse.Namespace) -> bool:
    52→    if args.status != "fixed":
    53→        return True
    54→    note = getattr(args, "note", None)
    55→    if validate_note_length(note):
    56→        return True
    57→    show_note_length_requirement(note)
    58→    return False
    59→
    60→
    61→def _update_living_plan_after_resolve(
    62→    *,
    63→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    64→    all_resolved: list[str],
    65→    attestation: str | None,
    66→) -> dict | None:
    67→    plan = None
    68→    try:
    69→        if not has_living_plan():
    70→            return None
    71→        plan = load_plan()
    72→        purged = purge_ids(plan, all_resolved)
    73→        append_log_entry(
    74→            plan,
    75→            "resolve",
    76→            issue_ids=all_resolved,
    77→            actor="user",
    78→            note=getattr(args, "note", None),
    79→            detail={"status": args.status, "attestation": attestation},
    80→        )
    81→        # Commit tracking: add to uncommitted on fix, remove on reopen
    82→        if args.status == "fixed":
    83→            add_uncommitted_issues(plan, all_resolved)
    84→        elif args.status == "open":
    85→            purge_uncommitted_ids(plan, all_resolved)
    86→        save_plan(plan)
    87→        if purged:
    88→            print(colorize(f"  Plan updated: {purged} item(s) removed from queue.", "dim"))
    89→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    90→        _logger.debug("plan update failed after resolve", exc_info=True)
    91→        print(colorize("  Warning: could not update living plan.", "yellow"), file=sys.stderr)
    92→    return plan
    93→
    94→
    95→def cmd_resolve(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    96→    """Resolve issue(s) matching one or more patterns."""
    97→    attestation = getattr(args, "attest", None)
    98→    _validate_resolve_inputs(args, attestation)
    99→    if not _validate_fixed_note(args):
   100→        return
   101→
   102→    state_file = state_path(args)
   103→    state = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   104→
   105→    if _check_queue_order_guard(state, args.patterns, args.status):
   106→        return
   107→
   108→    if args.status == "fixed":
   109→        require_triage_current_or_exit(
   110→            state=state,
   111→            bypass=bool(getattr(args, "force_resolve", False)),
   112→            attest=getattr(args, "attest", "") or "",
   113→        )
   114→
   115→    _enforce_batch_wontfix_confirmation(
   116→        state,
   117→        args,
   118→        attestation=attestation,
   119→        resolve_all_patterns_fn=_resolve_all_patterns,
   120→    )
   121→    prev = _previous_score_snapshot(state)
   122→    prev_subjective_scores = {
   123→        str(dim): (coerce_assessment_score(payload) or 0.0)
   124→        for dim, payload in (state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}).items()
   125→        if isinstance(dim, str)
   126→    }
   127→
   128→    all_resolved = _resolve_all_patterns(state, args, attestation=attestation)
   129→    if not all_resolved:
   130→        status_label = "resolved" if args.status == "open" else "open"
   131→        print(colorize(f"No {status_label} issues matching: {' '.join(args.patterns)}", "yellow"))
   132→        return
   133→
   134→    _save_state_or_exit(state, state_file)
   135→
   136→    plan = _update_living_plan_after_resolve(
   137→        args=args,
   138→        all_resolved=all_resolved,
   139→        attestation=attestation,
   140→    )
   141→
   142→    _print_resolve_summary(status=args.status, all_resolved=all_resolved)
   143→    _print_wontfix_batch_warning(
   144→        state,
   145→        status=args.status,
   146→        resolved_count=len(all_resolved),
   147→    )
   148→    show_score_with_plan_context(state, prev)
   149→    render_commit_guidance(state, plan, all_resolved, args.status)
   150→    _print_subjective_reset_hint(
   151→        args=args,
   152→        state=state,
   153→        all_resolved=all_resolved,
   154→        prev_subjective_scores=prev_subjective_scores,
   155→    )
   156→
   157→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   158→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   159→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
   160→        state,
   161→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="resolve"),
   162→    )
   163→    if narrative.get("milestone"):
   164→        print(colorize(f"  → {narrative['milestone']}", "green"))
   165→
   166→    next_command = _print_next_command(state)
   167→    _write_resolve_query_entry(
   168→        ResolveQueryContext(
   169→            patterns=args.patterns,
   170→            status=args.status,
   171→            resolved=all_resolved,
   172→            next_command=next_command,
   173→            prev_overall=prev.overall,
   174→            prev_objective=prev.objective,
   175→            prev_strict=prev.strict,
   176→            prev_verified=prev.verified,
   177→            attestation=attestation,
   178→            narrative=narrative,
   179→            state=state,
   180→        )
   181→    )
   182→
```

```
1→"""State-application helpers for resolve command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import logging
     7→
     8→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import (
    10→    write_query_best_effort as _write_query_best_effort,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
    13→from desloppify.engine.plan import has_living_plan, load_plan
    14→
    15→from .selection import ResolveQueryContext
    16→
    17→_logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    18→
    19→
    20→def _try_expand_cluster(pattern: str) -> list[str] | None:
    21→    """If pattern matches a cluster name, return its issue IDs."""
    22→    try:
    23→        if not has_living_plan():
    24→            return None
    25→        plan = load_plan()
    26→        cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(pattern)
    27→        if cluster and cluster.get("issue_ids"):
    28→            return list(cluster["issue_ids"])
    29→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    30→        _logger.debug("cluster expansion skipped for %r", pattern, exc_info=True)
    31→    return None
    32→
    33→
    34→def _resolve_all_patterns(
    35→    state: dict,
    36→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    37→    *,
    38→    attestation: str | None,
    39→) -> list[str]:
    40→    all_resolved: list[str] = []
    41→    for pattern in args.patterns:
    42→        # Check if pattern is a cluster name — expand to member IDs
    43→        cluster_ids = _try_expand_cluster(pattern)
    44→        if cluster_ids:
    45→            for fid in cluster_ids:
    46→                resolved = state_mod.resolve_issues(
    47→                    state,
    48→                    fid,
    49→                    args.status,
    50→                    args.note,
    51→                    attestation=attestation,
    52→                )
    53→                all_resolved.extend(resolved)
    54→            continue
    55→
    56→        resolved = state_mod.resolve_issues(
    57→            state,
    58→            pattern,
    59→            args.status,
    60→            args.note,
    61→            attestation=attestation,
    62→        )
    63→        all_resolved.extend(resolved)
    64→    return all_resolved
    65→
    66→def _write_resolve_query_entry(context: ResolveQueryContext) -> None:
    67→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(context.state)
    68→    _write_query_best_effort(
    69→        {
    70→            "command": "resolve",
    71→            "patterns": context.patterns,
    72→            "status": context.status,
    73→            "resolved": context.resolved,
    74→            "count": len(context.resolved),
    75→            "next_command": context.next_command,
    76→            "overall_score": scores.overall,
    77→            "objective_score": scores.objective,
    78→            "strict_score": scores.strict,
    79→            "verified_strict_score": scores.verified,
    80→            "prev_overall_score": context.prev_overall,
    81→            "prev_objective_score": context.prev_objective,
    82→            "prev_strict_score": context.prev_strict,
    83→            "prev_verified_strict_score": context.prev_verified,
    84→            "attestation": context.attestation,
    85→            "narrative": context.narrative,
    86→        },
    87→        context="resolve query payload update",
    88→    )
    89→
```

```
1→"""State resolution operations (match + resolve)."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import copy
     6→
     7→__all__ = [
     8→    "coerce_assessment_score",
     9→    "match_issues",
    10→    "resolve_issues",
    11→]
    12→
    13→from desloppify.base.text_utils import is_numeric
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import _matches_pattern
    15→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import (
    16→    StateModel,
    17→    ensure_state_defaults,
    18→    utc_now,
    19→    validate_state_invariants,
    20→)
    21→
    22→
    23→from desloppify.engine._state import _recompute_stats
    24→
    25→
    26→def coerce_assessment_score(value: object) -> float | None:
    27→    """Normalize a subjective assessment score payload to a 0-100 float.
    28→
    29→    Returns ``None`` when the value cannot be interpreted as a numeric score
    30→    (e.g. bools, non-numeric strings, missing keys).
    31→    """
    32→    if is_numeric(value):
    33→        return round(max(0.0, min(100.0, float(value))), 1)
    34→    if isinstance(value, dict):
    35→        raw = value.get("score")
    36→        if not is_numeric(raw):
    37→            return None
    38→        return round(max(0.0, min(100.0, float(raw))), 1)
    39→    return None
    40→
    41→
    42→def _mark_stale_assessments_on_review_resolve(
    43→    state: StateModel,
    44→    *,
    45→    status: str,
    46→    resolved_issues: list[dict],
    47→    now: str,
    48→) -> None:
    49→    """Mark subjective assessments as stale when review issues are resolved.
    50→
    51→    The assessment score is preserved (not zeroed) — only a fresh review import
    52→    should change dimension scores.  The stale marker tells the UI to prompt
    53→    for a re-review.
    54→    """
    55→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    56→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
    57→        return
    58→
    59→    touched_dimensions: set[str] = set()
    60→    for issue in resolved_issues:
    61→        if issue.get("detector") != "review":
    62→            continue
    63→        dimension = str(issue.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")).strip()
    64→        if dimension:
    65→            touched_dimensions.add(dimension)
    66→
    67→    for dimension in sorted(touched_dimensions):
    68→        if dimension not in assessments:
    69→            continue
    70→
    71→        payload = assessments.get(dimension)
    72→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
    73→            payload["needs_review_refresh"] = True
    74→            payload["refresh_reason"] = f"review_issue_{status}"
    75→            payload["stale_since"] = now
    76→        else:
    77→            assessments[dimension] = {
    78→                "score": coerce_assessment_score(payload) or 0.0,
    79→                "needs_review_refresh": True,
    80→                "refresh_reason": f"review_issue_{status}",
    81→                "stale_since": now,
    82→            }
    83→
    84→
    85→def match_issues(
    86→    state: StateModel, pattern: str, status_filter: str = "open"
    87→) -> list[dict]:
    88→    """Return issues matching *pattern* with the given status."""
    89→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
    90→    return [
    91→        issue
    92→        for issue_id, issue in state["issues"].items()
    93→        if not issue.get("suppressed")
    94→        if (status_filter == "all" or issue["status"] == status_filter)
    95→        and _matches_pattern(issue_id, issue, pattern)
    96→    ]
    97→
    98→
    99→def resolve_issues(
   100→    state: StateModel,
   101→    pattern: str,
   102→    status: str,
   103→    note: str | None = None,
   104→    attestation: str | None = None,
   105→) -> list[str]:
   106→    """Set issue status for matches and return affected issue IDs."""
   107→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   108→    now = utc_now()
   109→    resolved: list[str] = []
   110→    resolved_issues: list[dict] = []
   111→    status_filter = "all" if status == "open" else "open"
   112→    for issue in match_issues(state, pattern, status_filter=status_filter):
   113→        previous_status = str(issue.get("status", "open")).strip() or "open"
   114→        if status == "open" and previous_status == "open":
   115→            continue
   116→
   117→        extra_updates: dict[str, object] = {}
   118→        if status == "wontfix":
   119→            snapshot_scan_count = int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)
   120→            extra_updates["wontfix_scan_count"] = snapshot_scan_count
   121→            extra_updates["wontfix_snapshot"] = {
   122→                "captured_at": now,
   123→                "scan_count": snapshot_scan_count,
   124→                "tier": issue.get("tier"),
   125→                "confidence": issue.get("confidence"),
   126→                "detail": copy.deepcopy(issue.get("detail", {})),
   127→            }
   128→        if status == "open":
   129→            issue["reopen_count"] = int(issue.get("reopen_count", 0) or 0) + 1
   130→            issue.pop("wontfix_scan_count", None)
   131→            issue.pop("wontfix_snapshot", None)
   132→            previous_note = issue.get("note")
   133→            next_note = note if note is not None else previous_note
   134→            extra_updates["resolved_at"] = None
   135→            extra_updates["note"] = next_note
   136→            reopen_attestation = {
   137→                "kind": "manual_reopen",
   138→                "text": attestation or note,
   139→                "attested_at": now,
   140→                "scan_verified": False,
   141→            }
   142→            reopen_attestation["previous_status"] = previous_status
   143→            extra_updates["resolution_attestation"] = reopen_attestation
   144→
   145→        updates: dict[str, object] = {
   146→            "status": status,
   147→            "note": note,
   148→            "resolved_at": now,
   149→            "suppressed": False,
   150→            "suppressed_at": None,
   151→            "suppression_pattern": None,
   152→            "resolution_attestation": {
   153→                "kind": "manual",
   154→                "text": attestation,
   155→                "attested_at": now,
   156→                "scan_verified": False,
   157→            },
   158→        }
   159→        updates.update(extra_updates)
   160→        issue.update(updates)
   161→        resolved.append(issue["id"])
   162→        resolved_issues.append(issue)
   163→
   164→    _mark_stale_assessments_on_review_resolve(
   165→        state,
   166→        status=status,
   167→        resolved_issues=resolved_issues,
   168→        now=now,
   169→    )
   170→
   171→    _recompute_stats(state, scan_path=state.get("scan_path"))
   172→    validate_state_invariants(state)
   173→    return resolved
   174→
```

```
    70→class _NoAbbrevArgumentParser(argparse.ArgumentParser):
    71→    """Argparse parser variant that disables long-option abbreviation."""
    72→
    73→    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
    74→        kwargs.setdefault("allow_abbrev", False)
    75→        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)

    78→def _cli_version_string() -> str:
    79→    """Return the best available CLI version label."""

    82→    except PackageNotFoundError:
```

```
1→"""CLI parser group builder for plan command family."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→
     8→def add_plan_parser(sub) -> None:
     9→    p_plan = sub.add_parser(
    10→        "plan",
    11→        help="Living plan: generate, reorder, cluster, skip, note",
    12→        description="""\
    13→Manage the living plan — a persistent layer on top of the work queue.
    14→Track custom ordering, clusters, skips, and per-issue annotations.
    15→Run with no subcommand to generate a full prioritized markdown plan.""",
    16→        epilog="""\
    17→typical workflow:
    18→  desloppify scan                       # detect issues
    19→  desloppify plan                       # full prioritized markdown
    20→  desloppify plan queue                 # compact table of all items
    21→  desloppify plan cluster create ...    # group related issues
    22→  desloppify plan focus <cluster>       # narrow scope
    23→  desloppify next                       # work on the next item
    24→  desloppify plan resolve <id> --attest .. # mark as fixed
    25→
    26→patterns (used by reorder, skip, resolve, describe, note, etc.):
    27→  Patterns match issues by detector, file, ID prefix, glob, or name.
    28→  Cluster names also work as patterns — they expand to all member IDs.
    29→  Examples: "security", "src/foo.py", "unused::*React*", "my-cluster"
    30→
    31→subcommands:
    32→  show       Show plan metadata summary
    33→  queue      Compact table of upcoming queue items
    34→  reset      Reset plan to empty
    35→  reorder    Reposition issues or clusters in the queue
    36→  resolve    Mark issues as fixed (score movement + next-step)
    37→  describe   Set augmented description
    38→  note       Set note on issues
    39→  skip       Skip issues (temporary/permanent/false_positive)
    40→  unskip     Bring skipped issues back to queue
    41→  reopen     Reopen resolved issues
    42→  focus      Set or clear active cluster focus
    43→  cluster    Manage issue clusters
    44→  triage     Staged triage workflow (after review)""",
    45→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
    46→    )
    47→    p_plan.add_argument("--state", type=str, default=None, help="Path to state file")
    48→    p_plan.add_argument(
    49→        "--output", type=str, metavar="FILE", help="Write to file instead of stdout"
    50→    )
    51→
    52→    plan_sub = p_plan.add_subparsers(dest="plan_action")
    53→
    54→    # plan show
    55→    plan_sub.add_parser("show", help="Show plan metadata summary")
    56→
    57→    # plan queue
    58→    p_queue = plan_sub.add_parser("queue", help="Compact table of upcoming queue items")
    59→    p_queue.add_argument("--top", type=int, default=30, help="Max items (default: 30, 0=all)")
    60→    p_queue.add_argument("--cluster", type=str, default=None, metavar="NAME",
    61→                         help="Filter to a specific cluster")
    62→    p_queue.add_argument("--include-skipped", action="store_true",
    63→                         help="Include skipped items at end")
    64→    p_queue.add_argument("--sort", choices=["priority", "recent"], default="priority",
    65→                         help="Sort order (default: priority)")
    66→
    67→    # plan reset
    68→    plan_sub.add_parser("reset", help="Reset plan to empty")
    69→
    70→    # plan reorder <patterns> <position> [--target TARGET]
    71→    p_move = plan_sub.add_parser(
    72→        "reorder",
    73→        help="Reposition issues in the queue",
    74→        epilog="""\
    75→patterns accept issue IDs, detector names, file paths, globs, or cluster names.
    76→cluster names expand to all member IDs automatically.
    77→
    78→examples:
    79→  desloppify plan reorder security top                         # all issues from detector
    80→  desloppify plan reorder "unused::src/foo.ts::*" top          # glob pattern
    81→  desloppify plan reorder smells bottom                        # deprioritize
    82→  desloppify plan reorder my-cluster top                       # cluster members
    83→  desloppify plan reorder my-cluster unused top                # mix clusters + issues
    84→  desloppify plan reorder unused before -t security            # before a issue/cluster
    85→  desloppify plan reorder smells after -t my-cluster           # after a cluster

    87→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
```

## Summary: Individual Work Item Completion Flow in Desloppify

Based on my thorough exploration of the desloppify codebase, here's where and how individual work items/tasks/issues get marked as complete:

### Primary Command: `desloppify plan resolve`

**File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/override_handlers.py` (lines 478-576)

**Handler Function**: `cmd_plan_resolve(args)` 

This is the main entry point for marking individual issues as complete. The command dispatches through the plan subcommand system.

#### Command Usage:
```bash
desloppify plan resolve <issue_id_or_pattern> --note "explanation" --attest "I have actually ... and I am not gaming the score."
```

### Parser Definition

**File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/cli_support/parser_groups_plan.py` (lines 165-204)

The parser defines the `plan resolve` subcommand with these flags:
- `patterns` (positional): Issue ID(s), detector, file path, glob, or cluster name
- `--note`: Explanation of the fix (required for score integrity)
- `--attest`: Anti-gaming attestation (required; must include "I have actually" AND "not gaming")
- `--confirm`: Auto-generate attestation from note
- `--force-resolve`: Bypass triage guardrail when new issues are pending triage

### Workflow: How Issues Get Marked Complete

1. **User invokes command**:
   ```bash
   desloppify plan resolve "unused::src/foo.tsx::React" --attest "I have actually removed the import and I am not gaming the score."
   ```

2. **cmd_plan_resolve() processes** (override_handlers.py:478-576):
   - Validates note length and attestation format
   - Checks for triage dependencies (blocks synthetic triage workflow IDs)
   - Validates cluster completion guard (prevents bulk-completing small clusters)
   - Logs the "done" action to the plan file
   - Delegates to `cmd_resolve()` for actual state update

3. **cmd_resolve() updates state** (resolve/cmd.py:95-182):
   - Calls `_resolve_all_patterns()` to match and update issues
   - Updates the living plan (removes from queue, adds to commit tracking)
   - Shows score movement and next steps
   - Writes query entry for external tools

4. **Issue status is actually updated in state**:
   - **File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/resolution.py` (lines 99-173)
   - **Function**: `resolve_issues(state, pattern, status, note, attestation)`
   
   This function:
   - Matches issues by pattern
   - Sets `issue["status"] = "fixed"` 
   - Records `issue["resolved_at"] = now` (UTC timestamp)
   - Stores attestation in `issue["resolution_attestation"]` dict with:
     - `"kind": "manual"`
     - `"text": attestation`
     - `"attested_at": now`
     - `"scan_verified": False`
   - Clears suppression flags
   - Records the note
   - Recomputes stats and validates state invariants

### State Persistence

**File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/persistence.py`
- Issues are stored in state file (`.desloppify/state-{lang}.json`)
- State writes are atomic (temp file + os.replace) with `.json.bak` backups

### Living Plan Updates

**File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/plan.py`
- When issues are resolved, they are `purge_id()`'d from the queue
- Added to `"uncommitted"` list for commit tracking
- Logged with `append_log_entry()` recording the user action

### Related Commands for Completion Workflows

1. **`desloppify plan skip`** - Skip items instead of marking complete (lines 209-282)
   - `--permanent` (wontfix) - requires `--note` and `--attest`
   - `--false-positive` - requires `--attest`
   - `--reason` and `--review-after` for temporary skips

2. **`desloppify plan reopen`** - Reopen resolved issues (lines 332-381)

3. **`desloppify next`** - Show next highest-priority item to work on (next/cmd.py)

4. **`desloppify plan cluster`** - Manage grouped issues (can mark clusters as done)

### Anti-Gaming Safeguards

The resolve flow includes several integrity checks:
1. **Attestation validation** - Must contain both "I have actually" and "not gaming"
2. **Note length validation** - Prevents empty/minimal notes
3. **Triage dependency blocking** - Can't mark triage items complete out-of-order
4. **Cluster guard** - Blocks bulk-completing small clusters (≤10 items); requires individual marking
5. **Scan verification** - Attestation marked `scan_verified: False` until next scan validates

### Key Data Structures

- **Issue object** (state_mod.Issue):
  - `status`: "open" | "fixed" | "wontfix" | "false_positive"
  - `resolved_at`: ISO timestamp
  - `note`: User explanation
  - `resolution_attestation`: Dict with kind, text, attested_at, scan_verified
  - `id`: Unique issue identifier

- **Living plan** (engine/plan.py):
  - `queue_order`: List of open issue IDs
  - `skipped`: Dict of skipped issues
  - `log`: History of user actions
  - `uncommitted`: Issues resolved but not yet committed

This is a comprehensive system that tracks individual task completion with strong anti-gaming measures, living plan management, and persistent state updates.

```
1→"""CLI entry point: parse args, load shared context, dispatch command handlers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→import sys
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.cli_support.parser import create_parser as _create_parser
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import LangResolutionError, resolve_lang
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import CommandRuntime
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.registry import get_command_handlers
    13→from desloppify.base.config import load_config
    14→from desloppify.base.discovery.source import set_exclusions
    15→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import CommandError
    16→from desloppify.base.output.fallbacks import log_best_effort_failure
    17→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    18→from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_default_path, get_project_root
    19→from desloppify.base.registry import detector_names, on_detector_registered
    20→from desloppify.base.runtime_state import runtime_scope
    21→from desloppify.languages import available_langs
    22→from desloppify.state import load_state
    23→
    24→_DETECTOR_NAMES_CACHE: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    25→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    26→
    27→
    28→def _get_detector_names() -> list[str]:
    29→    """Return cached detector names, computing on first access."""
    30→    if "names" not in _DETECTOR_NAMES_CACHE:
    31→        _DETECTOR_NAMES_CACHE["names"] = detector_names()
    32→    return _DETECTOR_NAMES_CACHE["names"]
    33→
    34→
    35→def _invalidate_detector_names_cache() -> None:
    36→    """Invalidate detector-name cache when runtime registrations change."""
    37→    _DETECTOR_NAMES_CACHE.pop("names", None)
    38→
    39→
    40→on_detector_registered(_invalidate_detector_names_cache)
    41→
    42→
    43→def create_parser():
    44→    """Return the top-level argparse parser."""
    45→    return _create_parser(langs=available_langs(), detector_names=_get_detector_names())
    46→
    47→
    48→def _apply_persisted_exclusions(args, config: dict):
    49→    """Merge CLI --exclude with persisted config.exclude and apply globally."""
    50→    cli_exclusions = getattr(args, "exclude", None) or []
    51→    persisted = config.get("exclude", [])
    52→    combined = list(cli_exclusions) + [e for e in persisted if e not in cli_exclusions]
    53→    if not combined:
    54→        return
    55→    set_exclusions(combined)
    56→    if cli_exclusions:
    57→        print(
    58→            colorize(f"  Excluding: {', '.join(combined)}", "dim"),
    59→            file=sys.stderr,
    60→        )
    61→        return
    62→    print(
    63→        colorize(
    64→            f"  Excluding (from config): {', '.join(combined)}", "dim"
    65→        ),
    66→        file=sys.stderr,
    67→    )
    68→
    69→
    70→def _resolve_default_path(args) -> None:
    71→    """Fill args.path from detected language or default source path.
    72→
    73→    For the review command, the last scan path (stored in state) is used as the
    74→    default so that ``desloppify review --prepare`` works on the same scope as
    75→    the preceding scan even when the project files are not under ``src/``.
    76→    """
    77→    if getattr(args, "path", None) is not None:
    78→        return
    79→    runtime_root = get_project_root()
    80→    if getattr(args, "command", None) == "review":
    81→        try:
    82→            state_file = state_path(args)
    83→            if state_file:
    84→                saved = load_state(state_file)
    85→                saved_path = saved.get("scan_path")
    86→                if saved_path:
    87→                    args.path = str((runtime_root / saved_path).resolve())
    88→                    return
    89→        except (OSError, KeyError, ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError) as exc:
    90→            log_best_effort_failure(logger, "resolve default review path from saved state", exc)
    91→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
    92→    if lang:
    93→        args.path = str(runtime_root / lang.default_src)
    94→    else:
    95→        args.path = str(get_default_path())
    96→
    97→
    98→def _load_shared_runtime(args) -> None:
    99→    """Load config/state and attach shared objects to parsed args."""
   100→    config = load_config()
   101→
   102→    state_file = state_path(args)
   103→    state = load_state(state_file)
   104→    _apply_persisted_exclusions(args, config)
   105→
   106→    args.runtime = CommandRuntime(config=config, state=state, state_path=state_file)
   107→
   108→
   109→def _resolve_handler(command: str):
   110→    return get_command_handlers()[command]
   111→
   112→
   113→def _handle_help_command(args, parser) -> None:
   114→    """Handle explicit help command when present in parser config."""
   115→    topic = list(getattr(args, "topic", []) or [])
   116→    try:
   117→        parser.parse_args([*topic, "--help"])
   118→    except SystemExit:
   119→        return
   120→
   121→
   122→def main() -> None:
   123→    # Ensure Unicode output works on Windows terminals (cp1252 etc.)
   124→    for stream in (sys.stdout, sys.stderr):
   125→        if hasattr(stream, "reconfigure"):
   126→            try:
   127→                stream.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
   128→            except (AttributeError, OSError):
   129→                logger.debug(
   130→                    "Skipping stream reconfigure for %s (not supported)",
   131→                    getattr(stream, "name", "<stream>"),
   132→                )
   133→
   134→    parser = create_parser()
   135→    args = parser.parse_args()
   136→    if args.command == "help":
   137→        _handle_help_command(args, parser)
   138→        return
   139→
   140→    try:
   141→        with runtime_scope():
   142→            _resolve_default_path(args)
   143→            _load_shared_runtime(args)
   144→
   145→            handler = _resolve_handler(args.command)
   146→            handler(args)
   147→    except CommandError as exc:
   148→        print(colorize(f"  {exc.message}", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   149→        sys.exit(exc.exit_code)
   150→    except LangResolutionError as exc:
   151→        print(colorize(f"  {exc.message}", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   152→        sys.exit(1)
   153→    except KeyboardInterrupt:
   154→        print("\nInterrupted.")
   155→        sys.exit(1)
   156→
   157→
   158→if __name__ == "__main__":
   159→    main()
   160→
```

```
1→"""scan command: run all detectors, update persistent state, show diff."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import resolve_lang
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import query_file_path
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime_options import (
    10→    LangRuntimeOptionsError,
    11→    print_lang_runtime_options_error,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.base.config import target_strict_score_from_config
    14→from desloppify.app.commands.scan.artifacts import (

    16→    emit_scorecard_badge,
    17→)

    30→)
```
