    33→)
    34→from desloppify.app.commands.scan.reporting.summary import (  # noqa: F401
    35→    show_diff_summary,
    36→    show_score_delta,
    37→)
    38→from desloppify.app.commands.scan.workflow import (
    39→    ScanStateContractError,
    40→    merge_scan_results,
    41→    persist_reminder_history,
    42→    prepare_scan_runtime,
    43→    resolve_noise_snapshot,
    44→    run_scan_generation,
    45→)
    46→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    47→from desloppify.base.search.query import write_query
    48→
    49→from . import preflight as scan_preflight_mod
    50→
    51→
    52→def _print_scan_header(lang_label: str) -> None:
    53→    """Print the scan header line."""
    54→    print(colorize(f"\nDesloppify Scan{lang_label}\n", "bold"))
    55→
    56→
    57→def _print_scan_complete_banner() -> None:
    58→    """Print scan completion hint banner."""
    59→    lines = [
    60→        colorize("  Scan complete", "bold"),
    61→        colorize("  " + "─" * 50, "dim"),
    62→    ]
    63→    print("\n".join(lines))
    64→
    65→
    66→def _show_scan_visibility(noise, effective_include_slow: bool) -> None:
    67→    """Print fast-scan and noise budget visibility hints.
    68→
    69→    Side-effect only: conditionally prints scan-mode warnings to stdout.
    70→    All branches may be skipped (full scan, no noise budget hit, no hidden
    71→    issues), so the function can legitimately produce no output.
    72→    """
    73→    if not effective_include_slow:
    74→        print(colorize("  * Fast scan — slow phases (duplicates) skipped", "yellow"))
    75→    if noise.budget_warning:
    76→        print(colorize(f"  * {noise.budget_warning}", "yellow"))
    77→    if noise.hidden_total:
    78→        print(
    79→            colorize(
    80→                f"  * {noise.hidden_total} issues hidden (showing {noise.noise_budget}/detector). "
    81→                "Use `desloppify show <detector>` to see all.",
    82→                "dim",
    83→            )
    84→        )
    85→
    86→
    87→def _show_coverage_preflight(runtime) -> None:
    88→    """Print preflight warnings when scan coverage confidence is reduced."""
    89→    warnings = getattr(runtime, "coverage_warnings", []) or []
    90→    if not isinstance(warnings, list) or not warnings:
    91→        return
    92→
    93→    for entry in warnings:
    94→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
    95→            continue
    96→        summary = str(entry.get("summary", "")).strip()
    97→        impact = str(entry.get("impact", "")).strip()
    98→        remediation = str(entry.get("remediation", "")).strip()
    99→        detector = str(entry.get("detector", "")).strip() or "detector"
   100→
   101→        headline = summary or f"Coverage reduced for `{detector}`."
   102→        print(colorize(f"  * Coverage preflight: {headline}", "yellow"))
   103→        if impact:
   104→            print(colorize(f"    Repercussion: {impact}", "dim"))
   105→        if remediation:
   106→            print(colorize(f"    Fix: {remediation}", "dim"))
   107→
   108→
   109→def _print_plan_workflow_nudge(state: dict) -> None:
   110→    _print_plan_workflow_nudge_impl(state)
   111→
   112→
   113→def cmd_scan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   114→    """Run all detectors, update persistent state, show diff."""
   115→    scan_preflight_mod.scan_queue_preflight(args)
   116→    try:
   117→        runtime = prepare_scan_runtime(args)
   118→    except LangRuntimeOptionsError as exc:
   119→        lang_cfg = resolve_lang(args)
   120→        lang_name = lang_cfg.name if lang_cfg else "selected"
   121→        print_lang_runtime_options_error(exc, lang_name=lang_name)
   122→        raise SystemExit(2) from exc
   123→    except ScanStateContractError as exc:
   124→        print(colorize(f"  {exc}", "red"))
   125→        raise SystemExit(2) from exc
   126→    orchestrator = ScanOrchestrator(
   127→        runtime,
   128→        run_scan_generation_fn=run_scan_generation,
   129→        merge_scan_results_fn=merge_scan_results,
   130→        resolve_noise_snapshot_fn=resolve_noise_snapshot,
   131→        persist_reminder_history_fn=persist_reminder_history,
   132→    )
   133→    _print_scan_header(runtime.lang_label)
   134→    if runtime.reset_subjective_count > 0:
   135→        print(
   136→            colorize(
   137→                "  * Subjective reset "
   138→                f"{runtime.reset_subjective_count} subjective dimensions to 0",
   139→                "yellow",
   140→            )
   141→        )
   142→    if runtime.expired_manual_override_count > 0:
   143→        print(
   144→            colorize(
   145→                "  * Expired provisional manual-override assessments: "
   146→                f"{runtime.expired_manual_override_count} dimension(s) reset to 0. "
   147→                "Use trusted `review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` to replace them.",
   148→                "yellow",
   149→            )
   150→        )
   151→    _show_coverage_preflight(runtime)
   152→
   153→    issues, potentials, codebase_metrics = orchestrator.generate()
   154→    merge = orchestrator.merge(issues, potentials, codebase_metrics)
   155→    _print_scan_complete_banner()
   156→
   157→    noise = orchestrator.noise_snapshot()
   158→
   159→    target_value = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config)
   160→
   161→    show_diff_summary(merge.diff)
   162→    show_score_delta(
   163→        runtime.state,
   164→        merge.prev_overall,
   165→        merge.prev_objective,
   166→        merge.prev_strict,
   167→        merge.prev_verified,
   168→        target_strict=target_value,
   169→    )
   170→    # Nudge: if plan_start_scores was just seeded, tell the agent about the lifecycle.
   171→    _print_plan_workflow_nudge(runtime.state)
   172→    _show_scan_visibility(noise, runtime.effective_include_slow)
   173→    show_scorecard_subjective_measures(runtime.state)
   174→    show_score_model_breakdown(runtime.state)
   175→
   176→    new_dim_scores = runtime.state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   177→    if new_dim_scores and merge.prev_dim_scores:
   178→        show_dimension_deltas(merge.prev_dim_scores, new_dim_scores)
   179→
   180→    warnings, narrative = show_post_scan_analysis(
   181→        merge.diff,
   182→        runtime.state,
   183→        runtime.lang,
   184→        target_strict_score=target_value,
   185→    )
   186→    orchestrator.persist_reminders(narrative)
   187→
   188→    write_query(
   189→        build_scan_query_payload(
   190→            runtime.state,
   191→            runtime.config,
   192→            runtime.profile,
   193→            merge.diff,
   194→            warnings,
   195→            narrative,
   196→            merge,
   197→            noise,
   198→        ),
   199→        query_file=query_file_path(),
   200→    )
   201→
   202→    badge_path, _badge_result = emit_scorecard_badge(args, runtime.config, runtime.state)
   203→    print_llm_summary(runtime.state, badge_path, narrative, merge.diff)
   204→    auto_update_skill()
   205→
   206→
   207→__all__ = [
   208→    "cmd_scan",
   209→]
   210→

    47→class NextOptions:

    76→def _scorecard_subjective(
    77→    state: dict,
    78→    dim_scores: dict,
    79→) -> list[dict]:
    80→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""
    81→    return _scorecard_subjective_impl(state, dim_scores)
    82→
    83→
    84→def _low_subjective_dimensions(
    85→    state: dict,
    86→    dim_scores: dict,
    87→    *,
    88→    threshold: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    89→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:
    90→    """Return assessed scorecard-subjective entries below the threshold."""
    91→    low: list[tuple[str, float, int]] = []
    92→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):
    93→        if entry.get("placeholder"):
    94→            continue
    95→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    96→        if strict_val < threshold:
    97→            low.append(
    98→                (
    99→                    str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")),
   100→                    strict_val,
   101→                    int(entry.get("failing", 0)),
   102→                )
   103→            )
   104→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
   105→    return low
   106→
   107→
   108→def cmd_next(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   109→    """Show next highest-priority queue items."""
   110→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   111→    state = runtime.state
   112→    config = runtime.config
   113→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   114→        return
   115→
   116→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
   117→    if skill_warning:
   118→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
   119→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
   120→    if config_warning:
   121→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
   122→
   123→    print_triage_guardrail_info(state=state)
   124→    _get_items(args, state, config)
   125→
   126→
   127→def _resolve_cluster_focus(
   128→    plan_data: dict | None,
   129→    *,
   130→    cluster_arg: str | None,
   131→    scope: str | None,
   132→) -> str | None:
   133→    effective_cluster = cluster_arg
   134→    if plan_data and not cluster_arg and not scope:
   135→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
   136→        if active_cluster:
   137→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
   138→    return effective_cluster
   139→
   140→
   141→def _build_next_payload(
   142→    *,
   143→    queue: dict,
   144→    items: list[dict],
   145→    state: dict,
   146→    narrative: dict,
   147→    plan_data: dict | None,
   148→) -> dict:
   149→    payload = next_output_mod.build_query_payload(
   150→        queue, items, command="next", narrative=narrative, plan=plan_data
   151→    )
   152→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   153→    payload["overall_score"] = scores.overall
   154→    payload["objective_score"] = scores.objective
   155→    payload["strict_score"] = scores.strict
   156→    payload["scorecard_dimensions"] = scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   157→        state,
   158→        dim_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   159→    )
   160→    payload["subjective_measures"] = [
   161→        row for row in payload["scorecard_dimensions"] if row.get("subjective")
   162→    ]
   163→    return payload
   164→
   165→
   166→def _emit_requested_output(
   167→    opts: NextOptions,
   168→    payload: dict,
   169→    items: list[dict],
   170→) -> bool:
   171→    if opts.output_file:
   172→        if next_output_mod.write_output_file(
   173→            opts.output_file,
   174→            payload,
   175→            len(items),
   176→            safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   177→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   178→        ):
   179→            return True
   180→        raise SystemExit(1)
   181→
   182→    if next_output_mod.emit_non_terminal_output(opts.output_format, payload, items):
   183→        return True
   184→    return False
   185→
   186→
   187→def _plan_queue_context(
   188→    *,
   189→    state: dict,
   190→    plan_data: dict | None,
   191→    context=None,
   192→) -> tuple[float | None, QueueBreakdown | None]:
   193→    effective_plan = context.plan if context is not None else plan_data
   194→    plan_start_strict = get_plan_start_strict(effective_plan)
   195→    try:
   196→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan_data, context=context)
   197→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   198→        breakdown = None
   199→    return plan_start_strict, breakdown
   200→
   201→
   202→def _merge_potentials_safe(raw_potentials: dict | None) -> dict | None:
   203→    try:
   204→        return merge_potentials(raw_potentials) or None
   205→    except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError):
   206→        return raw_potentials or None
   207→
   208→
   209→def _get_items(args: argparse.Namespace, state: dict, config: dict) -> None:
   210→    opts = NextOptions.from_args(args)
   211→
   212→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config)
   213→
   214→    # Load the living plan
   215→    plan = load_plan()
   216→    plan_data: dict | None = None
   217→    if (
   218→        plan.get("queue_order")
   219→        or plan.get("overrides")
   220→        or plan.get("clusters")
   221→    ):
   222→        plan_data = plan
   223→
   224→    # Build unified context once — all downstream consumers agree on
   225→    # plan, target_strict, and subjective visibility policy.
   226→    ctx = queue_context(
   227→        state, config=config, plan=plan_data, target_strict=target_strict,
   228→    )
   229→
   230→    # Auto-scope to focus cluster if set and no explicit scope/cluster
   231→    effective_cluster = _resolve_cluster_focus(
   232→        plan_data,
   233→        cluster_arg=opts.cluster,
   234→        scope=opts.scope,
   235→    )
   236→
   237→    queue = build_work_queue(
   238→        state,
   239→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   240→            count=opts.count,
   241→            scope=opts.scope,
   242→            status=opts.status,
   243→            include_subjective=True,
   244→            subjective_threshold=target_strict,
   245→            explain=opts.explain,
   246→            include_skipped=opts.include_skipped,
   247→            cluster=effective_cluster,
   248→            context=ctx,
   249→        ),
   250→    )
   251→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   252→
   253→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   254→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   255→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   256→        state,
   257→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="next", plan=plan_data),
   258→    )
   259→
   260→    payload = _build_next_payload(
   261→        queue=queue,
   262→        items=items,
   263→        state=state,
   264→        narrative=narrative,
   265→        plan_data=plan_data,
   266→    )
   267→    write_query(payload)
   268→
   269→    if _emit_requested_output(opts, payload, items):
   270→        return
   271→
   272→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   273→    issues_scoped = state_mod.path_scoped_issues(
   274→        state.get("issues", {}),
   275→        state.get("scan_path"),
   276→    )
   277→
   278→    # Extract frozen plan-start score and queue breakdown for lifecycle display
   279→    plan_start_strict, breakdown = _plan_queue_context(
   280→        state=state,
   281→        plan_data=plan_data,
   282→        context=ctx,
   283→    )
   284→    queue_total = breakdown.queue_total if breakdown else 0
   285→
   286→    _render_queue_header(queue, opts.explain)
   287→    strict_score = state_mod.score_snapshot(state).strict
   288→    if _show_empty_queue(
   289→        queue,
   290→        strict_score,
   291→        plan_start_strict=plan_start_strict,
   292→        target_strict=target_strict,
   293→    ):
   294→        return
   295→
   296→    raw_potentials = state.get("potentials", {})
   297→    potentials = _merge_potentials_safe(raw_potentials)
   298→    next_render_mod.render_terminal_items(
   299→        items, dim_scores, issues_scoped, group=opts.group, explain=opts.explain,
   300→        potentials=potentials, plan=plan_data,
   301→        cluster_filter=effective_cluster,
   302→    )
   303→    next_nudges_mod.render_single_item_resolution_hint(items)
   304→    next_nudges_mod.render_uncommitted_reminder(plan_data)
   305→    next_nudges_mod.render_followup_nudges(
   306→        state,
   307→        dim_scores,
   308→        issues_scoped,
   309→        strict_score=strict_score,
   310→        target_strict_score=target_strict,
   311→        queue_total=queue_total,
   312→        plan_start_strict=plan_start_strict,
   313→        breakdown=breakdown,
   314→    )
   315→    print()
   316→
   317→    if items and plan_data:
   318→        print_user_message(
   319→            "Hey — the task details are printed above. Just start"
   320→            " working on it. When you're done, resolve it with"
   321→            " `desloppify plan resolve` and then run `desloppify next`"
   322→            " again for the next one. If this doesn't seem like the"
   323→            " right priority, run `desloppify plan show` to see the"
   324→            " full queue and reorder. Don't stop until you're done"
   325→            " — unless I've told you otherwise, just keep going."
   326→        )
   327→

    43→def cmd_plan_output(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    44→    """Generate a prioritized markdown plan from state."""
    45→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    46→    state = runtime.state
    47→
    48→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    49→        return
    50→
    51→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(runtime.config)
    52→    if config_warning:
    53→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    54→
    55→    plan_md = planning_mod.generate_plan_md(state)
    56→    next_command = "desloppify next --count 20"
    57→
    58→    output = getattr(args, "output", None)
    59→    if output:
    60→        try:
    61→            safe_write_text(output, plan_md)
    62→            print(colorize(f"Plan written to {output}", "green"))
    63→            print_agent_plan(
    64→                ["Inspect and execute the generated plan."],
    65→                next_command=next_command,
    66→            )
    67→        except OSError as e:
    68→            warn_best_effort(f"Could not write plan to {output}: {e}")
    69→    else:
    70→        print(plan_md)
    71→        print()
    72→        print_agent_plan(
    73→            ["Start from the top-ranked action in this plan."],
    74→            next_command=next_command,
    75→        )
    76→
    77→
    78→def _cmd_plan_generate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    79→    """Generate the prioritized markdown plan (existing behavior)."""
    80→    cmd_plan_output(args)
    81→
    82→
    83→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    84→    """Show plan metadata summary."""
    85→    plan = load_plan()
    86→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    87→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    88→    total_skipped = len(skipped)
    89→    kind_counts = {
    90→        kind: sum(1 for entry in skipped.values() if entry.get("kind") == kind)
    91→        for kind in USER_SKIP_KINDS
    92→    }
    93→    temp_count = kind_counts["temporary"]
    94→    perm_count = kind_counts["permanent"]
    95→    fp_count = kind_counts["false_positive"]
    96→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    97→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    98→    superseded = len(plan.get("superseded", {}))
    99→
   100→    described, noted = annotation_counts(plan)
   101→
   102→    print(colorize("  Living Plan Status", "bold"))
   103→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 40, "dim"))
   104→    print(f"  Queue:            {ordered} items prioritized")
   105→    if total_skipped:
   106→        print(f"  Skipped:          {total_skipped} (temp: {temp_count}, wontfix: {perm_count}, fp: {fp_count})")
   107→    else:
   108→        print("  Skipped:          0")
   109→    print(f"  Clusters:         {len(clusters)}")
   110→    if clusters:
   111→        for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   112→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   113→            member_count = len(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   114→            marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
   115→            desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
   116→            print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{desc_str}{marker}")
   117→    if described or noted:
   118→        print(f"  Annotations:      {described} described, {noted} noted")
   119→    if active:
   120→        print(f"  Focus:            {active}")
   121→    if superseded:
   122→        print(f"  Disappeared:      {superseded} (resolved or removed since last scan)")
   123→
   124→    # Commit tracking summary
   125→    _cfg = load_config()
   126→    if _cfg.get("commit_tracking_enabled", True):
   127→        ct = commit_tracking_summary(plan)
   128→        if ct["total"] > 0:
   129→            pr_num = _cfg.get("commit_pr", 0)
   130→            pr_str = f"  PR: #{pr_num}" if pr_num else ""
   131→            print(
   132→                f"  Commit tracking:  {ct['uncommitted']} uncommitted, "
   133→                f"{ct['committed']} committed ({ct['total']} issues){pr_str}"
   134→            )
   135→
   136→
   137→def _cmd_plan_reset(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   138→    """Reset the plan to empty."""
   139→    plan = load_plan()
   140→    queue_len = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   141→    cluster_count = len(plan.get("clusters", {}))
   142→    reset_plan(plan)
   143→    append_log_entry(
   144→        plan, "reset", actor="user",
   145→        detail={"previous_queue_size": queue_len, "previous_cluster_count": cluster_count},
   146→    )
   147→    save_plan(plan)
   148→    print(colorize("  Plan reset to empty.", "green"))
   149→
   150→
   151→_PLAN_ACTION_HANDLERS = {
   152→    "show": _cmd_plan_show,
   153→    "queue": cmd_plan_queue,
   154→    "reset": _cmd_plan_reset,
   155→    "reorder": cmd_plan_reorder,
   156→    "describe": cmd_plan_describe,
   157→    "resolve": cmd_plan_resolve,
   158→    "note": cmd_plan_note,
   159→    "focus": cmd_plan_focus,
   160→    "skip": cmd_plan_skip,
   161→    "unskip": cmd_plan_unskip,
   162→    "reopen": cmd_plan_reopen,
   163→    "cluster": cmd_cluster_dispatch,
   164→    "triage": cmd_plan_triage,
   165→    "commit-log": cmd_commit_log_dispatch,
   166→}
   167→
   168→
   169→def cmd_plan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   170→    """Dispatch plan subcommand or generate markdown output."""
   171→    plan_action = getattr(args, "plan_action", None)
   172→    if plan_action is None:
   173→        _cmd_plan_generate(args)
   174→        return
   175→
   176→    handler = _PLAN_ACTION_HANDLERS.get(plan_action)
   177→    if handler is None:
   178→        print(f"Unknown plan action: {plan_action}")
   179→        return
   180→    handler(args)
   181→
   182→__all__ = ["cmd_plan", "cmd_plan_output"]
   183→

    41→    """All user-facing show command options extracted once from argparse."""
    42→
    43→    pattern_raw: str = ""
    44→    show_code: bool = False
    45→    chronic: bool = False
    46→    no_budget: bool = False
    47→    output_file: str | None = None
    48→    top: int = 20
    49→
    50→    @classmethod
    51→    def from_args(cls, args: argparse.Namespace) -> ShowOptions:
    52→        return cls(
    53→            pattern_raw=str(getattr(args, "pattern", "") or ""),
    54→            show_code=bool(getattr(args, "code", False)),
    55→            chronic=bool(getattr(args, "chronic", False)),
    56→            no_budget=bool(getattr(args, "no_budget", False)),
    57→            output_file=getattr(args, "output", None),
    58→            top=int(getattr(args, "top", 20) or 20),
    59→        )
    60→
    61→
    62→def _handle_special_entity_views(
    63→    *,
    64→    entity,
    65→    state: dict,
    66→    config: dict,
    67→    lang_name: str | None,
    68→    pattern_raw: str,
    69→) -> bool:
    70→    if entity.kind == "special_view" and entity.pattern.strip().lower() == "concerns":
    71→        _show_concerns(state, lang_name)
    72→        return True
    73→    if entity.kind == "dimension" and entity.is_subjective:
    74→        _render_subjective_dimension(state, config, entity, pattern_raw)
    75→        return True
    76→    return False
    77→
    78→
    79→def _load_entity_matches(
    80→    *,
    81→    state: dict,
    82→    entity,
    83→    pattern: str,
    84→    status_filter: str,
    85→    scope: str | None,
    86→    chronic: bool,
    87→) -> tuple[str, list[dict]] | None:
    88→    if entity.kind != "dimension" or entity.is_subjective:
    89→        return pattern, load_matches(
    90→            state, scope=scope, status_filter=status_filter, chronic=chronic
    91→        )
    92→    matches = _load_dimension_issues(state, entity, status_filter)
    93→    if not matches:
    94→        _render_clean_mechanical_dimension(state, entity)
    95→        return None
    96→    return entity.display_name, matches
    97→
    98→
    99→def _active_plan_or_none() -> dict | None:
   100→    try:
   101→        plan = load_plan()
   102→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   103→        return None
   104→    if plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("clusters"):
   105→        return plan
   106→    return None
   107→
   108→
   109→def cmd_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   110→    """Show all issues for a file, directory, detector, or pattern."""
   111→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   112→    state = runtime.state
   113→    config = runtime.config
   114→
   115→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   116→        return
   117→
   118→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
   119→    if skill_warning:
   120→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
   121→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
   122→    if config_warning:
   123→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
   124→
   125→    print_triage_guardrail_info(state=state)
   126→
   127→    opts = ShowOptions.from_args(args)
   128→
   129→    ok, pattern, status_filter, scope = resolve_show_scope(args)
   130→    if not ok or pattern is None:
   131→        return
   132→
   133→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   134→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   135→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   136→        state,
   137→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="show"),
   138→    )
   139→
   140→    entity = resolve_entity(pattern, state)
   141→    if _handle_special_entity_views(
   142→        entity=entity,
   143→        state=state,
   144→        config=config,
   145→        lang_name=lang_name,
   146→        pattern_raw=opts.pattern_raw,
   147→    ):
   148→        return
   149→
   150→    match_result = _load_entity_matches(
   151→        state=state,
   152→        entity=entity,
   153→        pattern=pattern,
   154→        status_filter=status_filter,
   155→        scope=scope,
   156→        chronic=opts.chronic,
   157→    )
   158→    if match_result is None:
   159→        return
   160→    pattern, matches = match_result
   161→
   162→    if not matches:
   163→        _render_no_matches(entity, pattern, status_filter, narrative, state, config)
   164→        return
   165→
   166→    (
   167→        surfaced_matches,
   168→        hidden_by_detector,
   169→        noise_budget,
   170→        global_noise_budget,
   171→        budget_warning,
   172→    ) = resolve_noise(
   173→        config,
   174→        matches,
   175→        no_budget=opts.no_budget,
   176→    )
   177→    hidden_total = sum(hidden_by_detector.values())
   178→
   179→    payload = build_show_payload(
   180→        surfaced_matches,
   181→        pattern,
   182→        status_filter,
   183→        ShowPayloadMeta(
   184→            total_matches=len(matches),
   185→            hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   186→            noise_budget=noise_budget,
   187→            global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   188→        ),
   189→    )
   190→    write_query({"command": "show", **payload, "narrative": narrative})
   191→
   192→    if opts.output_file:
   193→        if write_show_output_file(opts.output_file, payload, len(surfaced_matches)):
   194→            return
   195→        raise SystemExit(1)
   196→
   197→    render_issues(
   198→        surfaced_matches,
   199→        pattern=pattern,
   200→        status_filter=status_filter,
   201→        show_code=opts.show_code,
   202→        top=opts.top,
   203→        hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   204→        hidden_total=hidden_total,
   205→        noise_budget=noise_budget,
   206→        global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   207→        budget_warning=budget_warning,
   208→    )
   209→    plan_active = _active_plan_or_none()
   210→    show_agent_plan(narrative, surfaced_matches, plan=plan_active)
   211→    show_subjective_followup(
   212→        state,
   213→        target_strict_score_from_config(config),
   214→    )
   215→
   216→    _render_subjective_views_guide(entity)
   217→
   218→
   219→__all__ = ["cmd_show"]
   220→

     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→

     8→from desloppify.app.commands._fix_preview import show_fix_dry_run_samples

    12→    _apply_and_report,
    13→    _detect,
    14→    _print_fix_summary,
    15→    _report_dry_run,
    16→    _warn_uncommitted_changes,
    17→)

    19→
    20→
    21→def cmd_fix(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    22→    """Auto-fix mechanical issues."""
    23→    fixer_name = args.fixer
    24→
    25→    dry_run = getattr(args, "dry_run", False)
    26→    path = Path(args.path)
    27→
    28→    lang, fixer = _load_fixer(args, fixer_name)
    29→
    30→    if not dry_run:
    31→        _warn_uncommitted_changes()
    32→    entries = _detect(fixer, path)
    33→    if not entries:
    34→        print(colorize(f"No {fixer.label} found.", "green"))
    35→        return
    36→
    37→    raw = fixer.fix(entries, dry_run=dry_run)
    38→    results = raw.entries
    39→    skip_reasons = raw.skip_reasons
    40→    total_items = sum(len(r["removed"]) for r in results)
    41→    total_lines = sum(r.get("lines_removed", 0) for r in results)
    42→    _print_fix_summary(fixer, results, total_items, total_lines, dry_run)
    43→
    44→    if dry_run and results:
    45→        show_fix_dry_run_samples(entries, results)
    46→
    47→    if not dry_run:
    48→        _apply_and_report(
    49→            args,
    50→            path,
    51→            fixer,
    52→            fixer_name,
    53→            entries,
    54→            results,
    55→            total_items,
    56→            lang,
    57→            skip_reasons,
    58→        )
    59→    else:
    60→        _report_dry_run(args, fixer_name, entries, results, total_items)
    61→    print()
    62→

     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.guardrails import print_triage_guardrail_info
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
     9→    QueueBreakdown,

    11→)
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    13→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
    14→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize, print_table
    15→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import (
    16→    QueueBuildOptions,
    17→    build_work_queue,
    18→)
    19→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import collapse_clusters
    20→from desloppify.engine.plan import compute_new_issue_ids, load_plan
    21→
    22→
    23→def _truncate(text: str, width: int) -> str:
    24→    if len(text) <= width:
    25→        return text
    26→    return text[: width - 1] + "\u2026"
    27→
    28→
    29→def _print_queue_header(
    30→    *,
    31→    items: list[dict],
    32→    include_skipped: bool,
    33→    plan: dict,
    34→    new_count: int = 0,
    35→) -> None:
    36→    total = len(items)
    37→    skipped_count = sum(1 for it in items if it.get("plan_skipped"))
    38→    non_skipped = total - skipped_count
    39→    plan_skipped_total = len(plan.get("skipped", {}))
    40→
    41→    # Count subjective items in the visible list
    42→    subjective = sum(
    43→        1 for it in items if it.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension"
    44→    )
    45→
    46→    # Count plan-ordered items (minus skipped)
    47→    queue_order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    48→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    49→    plan_ordered = sum(1 for fid in queue_order if fid not in skipped_ids)
    50→
    51→    breakdown = QueueBreakdown(
    52→        queue_total=non_skipped,
    53→        plan_ordered=plan_ordered,
    54→        skipped=plan_skipped_total,
    55→        subjective=subjective,
    56→    )
    57→    headline = format_queue_headline(breakdown)
    58→    new_suffix = f"  ({new_count} new this scan)" if new_count > 0 else ""
    59→    print(colorize(f"\n  {headline}{new_suffix}", "bold"))
    60→
    61→    focus = plan.get("active_cluster")
    62→    if focus:
    63→        print(colorize(f"  Focus: {focus}", "cyan"))
    64→
    65→    if include_skipped or skipped_count != 0:
    66→        return
    67→    if not plan_skipped_total:
    68→        return
    69→    print(
    70→        colorize(
    71→            f"  ({plan_skipped_total} skipped item{'s' if plan_skipped_total != 1 else ''}"
    72→            " hidden — use --include-skipped)",
    73→            "dim",
    74→        )
    75→    )
    76→
    77→
    78→def _queue_display_items(items: list[dict], *, top: int) -> list[dict]:
    79→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
    80→        return items[:top]
    81→    return items
    82→
    83→
    84→def _build_rows(display_items: list[dict], new_ids: set[str] | None = None) -> list[list[str]]:
    85→    rows: list[list[str]] = []
    86→    _new = new_ids or set()
    87→    for idx, item in enumerate(display_items, 1):
    88→        pos = str(idx)
    89→        kind = item.get("kind", "issue")
    90→
    91→        if kind == "workflow_stage":
    92→            blocked = item.get("is_blocked", False)
    93→            blocked_tag = " [blocked]" if blocked else ""
    94→            conf_str = "—"
    95→            detector = "planning"
    96→            summary = f"[TRIAGE] {item.get('summary', '')}{blocked_tag}"
    97→            cluster_name = ""
    98→        elif kind == "workflow_action":
    99→            conf_str = "—"
   100→            detector = "workflow"
   101→            summary = item.get("summary", "")
   102→            cluster_name = ""
   103→        elif kind == "cluster":
   104→            conf_str = item.get("confidence", "high")
   105→            member_count = item.get("member_count", 0)
   106→            detector = item.get("detector", "cluster")
   107→            new_in_cluster = sum(
   108→                1 for m in item.get("members", [])
   109→                if m.get("id") in _new
   110→            )
   111→            new_tag = f" (+{new_in_cluster} new)" if new_in_cluster else ""
   112→            summary = f"[{member_count} items{new_tag}] {item.get('summary', '')}"
   113→            cluster_name = item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id", ""))
   114→        else:
   115→            conf_str = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   116→            detector = item.get("detector", "")
   117→            summary = item.get("summary", "")
   118→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   119→            cluster_name = plan_cluster.get("name", "") if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else ""
   120→
   121→        prefix = "* " if item.get("id") in _new else ""
   122→        suffix = " [skip]" if item.get("plan_skipped") else ""
   123→        summary_display = _truncate(prefix + summary, 48) + suffix
   124→        rows.append([pos, conf_str, detector, summary_display, cluster_name])
   125→    return rows
   126→
   127→
   128→def cmd_plan_queue(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   129→    """Render a compact table of all upcoming queue items."""
   130→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   131→    state = runtime.state
   132→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   133→        return
   134→
   135→    top = getattr(args, "top", 30)
   136→    cluster_filter = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
   137→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
   138→
   139→    plan = load_plan()
   140→    print_triage_guardrail_info(plan=plan, state=state)
   141→
   142→    effective_cluster = cluster_filter
   143→    if not cluster_filter:
   144→        active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
   145→        if active_cluster:
   146→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
   147→
   148→    queue = build_work_queue(
   149→        state,
   150→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   151→            count=None,
   152→            status="open",
   153→            include_subjective=True,
   154→            plan=plan,
   155→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
   156→            cluster=effective_cluster,
   157→        ),
   158→    )
   159→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   160→    # Collapse auto-clusters into display meta-items
   161→    if plan and not effective_cluster and not plan.get("active_cluster"):
   162→        items = collapse_clusters(items, plan)
   163→
   164→    sort_by = getattr(args, "sort", "priority")
   165→    all_new_ids: set[str] = queue.get("new_ids", set())
   166→    # Merge review-based new issue IDs (since last triage)
   167→    review_new_ids = compute_new_issue_ids(plan, state)
   168→    all_new_ids = all_new_ids | review_new_ids
   169→    item_ids = {it.get("id") for it in items}
   170→    new_ids = all_new_ids & item_ids
   171→
   172→    if sort_by == "recent":
   173→        items = sorted(items, key=lambda it: it.get("first_seen", ""), reverse=True)
   174→
   175→    _print_queue_header(
   176→        items=items,
   177→        include_skipped=include_skipped,
   178→        plan=plan,
   179→        new_count=len(new_ids),
   180→    )
   181→
   182→    if not items:
   183→        print(colorize("\n  Queue is empty.", "green"))
   184→        return
   185→
   186→    # Determine which items to show
   187→    display_items = _queue_display_items(items, top=top)
   188→    headers = ["#", "Confidence", "Detector", "Summary", "Cluster"]
   189→    rows = _build_rows(display_items, new_ids=new_ids)
   190→
   191→    print()
   192→    widths = [4, 4, 12, 50, 16]
   193→    print_table(headers, rows, widths=widths)
   194→
   195→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
   196→        remaining = len(items) - top
   197→        print(colorize(
   198→            f"\n  ... and {remaining} more (use --top 0 to show all)", "dim"
   199→        ))
   200→    print()
   201→
   202→
   203→__all__ = ["cmd_plan_queue"]
   204→

    27→
    28→
    29→def render_uncommitted_reminder(plan: dict | None) -> None:
    30→    """Show a subtle reminder if there are uncommitted resolved issues."""
    31→    if plan is None:
    32→        return
    33→    try:
    34→        config = load_config()
    35→        if not config.get("commit_tracking_enabled", True):
    36→            return
    37→
    38→        uncommitted = plan.get("uncommitted_issues", [])
    39→        if not uncommitted:
    40→            return
    41→
    42→        count = len(uncommitted)
    43→        print(colorize(
    44→            f"\n  {count} resolved issue{'s' if count != 1 else ''} uncommitted"
    45→            " — `desloppify plan commit-log` to review",
    46→            "dim",
    47→        ))
    48→    except (ImportError, OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError) as exc:
    49→        log(f"  uncommitted reminder skipped: {exc}")
    50→
    51→
    52→def render_single_item_resolution_hint(items: list[dict]) -> None:
    53→    if len(items) != 1:
    54→        return
    55→    kind = items[0].get("kind", "issue")
    56→    if kind in ("cluster", "workflow_stage", "workflow_action"):
    57→        return  # These kinds have their own resolution hints
    58→    if kind != "issue":
    59→        return
    60→    item = items[0]
    61→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
    62→    if detector_name == "subjective_review":
    63→        print(colorize("\n  Review with:", "dim"))
    64→        primary = item.get(
    65→            "primary_command", "desloppify show subjective"
    66→        )
    67→        print(f"    {primary}")
    68→        if is_holistic_subjective_issue(item):
    69→            print("    desloppify review --prepare")
    70→        return
    71→
    72→    primary = item.get("primary_command", "")
    73→    if is_auto_fix_command(primary):
    74→        print(colorize("\n  Fix with:", "dim"))
    75→        print(f"    {primary}")
    76→        print(colorize("  Or resolve individually:", "dim"))
    77→    else:
    78→        print(colorize("\n  Resolve with:", "dim"))
    79→
    80→    print(
    81→        f'    desloppify plan resolve "{item["id"]}" --note "<what you did>" --confirm'
    82→    )
    83→    print(
    84→        f'    desloppify plan skip --permanent "{item["id"]}" --note "<why>" '
    85→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
    86→    )
    87→
    88→
    89→def _render_frozen_queue_status(
    90→    *,
    91→    strict_score: float | None,
    92→    queue_total: int,
    93→    plan_start_strict: float | None,
    94→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown | None,
    95→) -> bool:
    96→    if queue_total <= 0 or plan_start_strict is None:
    97→        return False
    98→    if breakdown is not None:
    99→        block = format_queue_block(
   100→            breakdown, frozen_score=plan_start_strict, live_score=strict_score
   101→        )
   102→        print()
   103→        for text, style in block:
   104→            print(colorize(text, style))
   105→        print(colorize(
   106→            "  Score will not update until the queue is clear and you run `desloppify scan`.",
   107→            "dim",
   108→        ))
   109→        return True
   110→
   111→    delta_str = ""
   112→    if strict_score is not None:
   113→        delta_str = format_plan_delta(strict_score, plan_start_strict)
   114→    if delta_str:
   115→        score_line = (
   116→            f"\n  Score: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 "
   117→            f"(plan start: {plan_start_strict:.1f}, {delta_str})"
   118→        )
   119→    else:
   120→        score_line = f"\n  Score (frozen at plan start): strict {plan_start_strict:.1f}/100"
   121→    print(colorize(score_line, "cyan"))
   122→    print(
   123→        colorize(
   124→            f"  Queue: {queue_total} item{'s' if queue_total != 1 else ''}"
   125→            " remaining. Score will not update until the queue is clear and you run `desloppify scan`.",
   126→            "dim",
   127→        )
   128→    )
   129→    return True
   130→
   131→
   132→def _render_north_star(
   133→    *,
   134→    strict_score: float | None,
   135→    target_strict_score: float,
   136→) -> None:
   137→    if strict_score is None:
   138→        return
   139→    gap = round(float(target_strict_score) - float(strict_score), 1)
   140→    if gap > 0:
   141→        print(
   142→            colorize(
   143→                f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 → target {target_strict_score:.1f} (+{gap:.1f} needed)",
   144→                "cyan",
   145→            )
   146→        )
   147→        return
   148→    print(
   149→        colorize(
   150→            f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 meets target {target_strict_score:.1f}",
   151→            "green",
   152→        )
   153→    )
   154→
   155→
   156→def _render_live_queue_block(
   157→    *,
   158→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown | None,
   159→    queue_total: int,
   160→    plan_start_strict: float | None,
   161→) -> None:
   162→    if breakdown is None or queue_total <= 0 or plan_start_strict is not None:
   163→        return
   164→    block = format_queue_block(breakdown)
   165→    for text, style in block:
   166→        print(colorize(text, style))
   167→
   168→
   169→def _objective_remaining(
   170→    queue_total: int,
   171→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown | None,
   172→) -> int:
   173→    if breakdown is None:
   174→        return max(0, queue_total)
   175→    return max(0, breakdown.queue_total - breakdown.subjective)
   176→
   177→
   178→def _render_subjective_bottleneck(dim_scores: dict) -> None:
   179→    try:
   180→        health_breakdown = compute_health_breakdown(dim_scores)
   181→        subjective_drag = sum(
   182→            float(e.get("overall_drag", 0) or 0)
   183→            for e in health_breakdown.get("entries", [])
   184→            if isinstance(e, dict) and e.get("component") == "subjective"
   185→        )
   186→        mechanical_drag = sum(
   187→            float(e.get("overall_drag", 0) or 0)
   188→            for e in health_breakdown.get("entries", [])
   189→            if isinstance(e, dict) and e.get("component") != "subjective"
   190→        )
   191→        if subjective_drag <= mechanical_drag or subjective_drag <= 5.0:
   192→            return
   193→        print(colorize(
   194→            f"\n  Subjective dimensions are the main bottleneck "
   195→            f"(-{subjective_drag:.0f} pts vs -{mechanical_drag:.0f} pts mechanical).",
   196→            "yellow",
   197→        ))
   198→        print(colorize(
   199→            "  Code fixes alone won't close the gap — run "
   200→            "`desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` "
   201→            "to re-score.",
   202→            "yellow",
   203→        ))
   204→    except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError, KeyError) as exc:
   205→        log(f"  subjective bottleneck banner skipped: {exc}")
   206→
   207→
   208→def _subjective_summary_parts(
   209→    *,
   210→    followup,
   211→    unassessed_subjective: list[str],
   212→    subjective_entries: list[dict],
   213→    issues_scoped: dict,
   214→    coverage_open: int,
   215→) -> list[str]:
   216→    parts: list[str] = []
   217→    low_dims = len(followup.low_assessed)
   218→    unassessed_count = len(unassessed_subjective)
   219→    stale_count = sum(1 for entry in subjective_entries if entry.get("stale"))
   220→    open_review_count = sum(
   221→        1
   222→        for issue in issues_scoped.values()
   223→        if issue.get("status") == "open" and issue.get("detector") == "review"
   224→    )
   225→    if low_dims:
   226→        parts.append(f"{low_dims} dimension{'s' if low_dims != 1 else ''} below target")
   227→    if stale_count:
   228→        parts.append(f"{stale_count} stale")
   229→    if unassessed_count:
   230→        parts.append(f"{unassessed_count} unassessed")
   231→    if open_review_count:
   232→        parts.append(
   233→            f"{open_review_count} review issue{'s' if open_review_count != 1 else ''} open"
   234→        )
   235→    if coverage_open > 0:
   236→        parts.append(f"{coverage_open} file{'s' if coverage_open != 1 else ''} need review")
   237→    return parts
   238→
   239→
   240→def render_followup_nudges(
   241→    state: dict,
   242→    dim_scores: dict,
   243→    issues_scoped: dict,
   244→    *,
   245→    strict_score: float | None,
   246→    target_strict_score: float,
   247→    queue_total: int = 0,
   248→    plan_start_strict: float | None = None,
   249→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown | None = None,
   250→) -> None:
   251→    subjective_threshold = target_strict_score
   252→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
   253→    followup = build_subjective_followup(
   254→        state,
   255→        subjective_entries,
   256→        threshold=subjective_threshold,
   257→        max_quality_items=3,
   258→        max_integrity_items=5,
   259→    )
   260→    unassessed_subjective = unassessed_subjective_dimensions(dim_scores)
   261→    rendered_frozen = _render_frozen_queue_status(
   262→        strict_score=strict_score,
   263→        queue_total=queue_total,
   264→        plan_start_strict=plan_start_strict,
   265→        breakdown=breakdown,
   266→    )
   267→    if not rendered_frozen:
   268→        _render_north_star(
   269→            strict_score=strict_score,
   270→            target_strict_score=target_strict_score,
   271→        )
   272→    _render_live_queue_block(
   273→        breakdown=breakdown,
   274→        queue_total=queue_total,
   275→        plan_start_strict=plan_start_strict,
   276→    )
   277→
   278→    # Subjective bottleneck banner — only shown when the objective queue is
   279→    # clear.  While objective items remain, the queue is the single authority
   280→    # on what to work on next; no need to distract with subjective advice.
   281→    objective_remaining = _objective_remaining(queue_total, breakdown)
   282→    if strict_score is not None and dim_scores and objective_remaining <= 0:
   283→        _render_subjective_bottleneck(dim_scores)
   284→
   285→    # Integrity penalty/warn lines preserved (anti-gaming safeguard, must remain visible).
   286→    for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
   287→        print(colorize(f"\n  {message}", style))
   288→
   289→    # Rescan nudge after structural work
   290→    if queue_total > 10:
   291→        print(colorize(
   292→            "\n  Tip: after structural fixes (splitting files, moving code), rescan to "
   293→            "let cascade effects settle: `desloppify scan --path .`",
   294→            "dim",
   295→        ))
   296→
   297→    # Collapsed subjective summary.
   298→    coverage_open, _coverage_reasons, _holistic_reasons = subjective_coverage_breakdown(
   299→        issues_scoped
   300→    )
   301→    parts = _subjective_summary_parts(
   302→        followup=followup,
   303→        unassessed_subjective=unassessed_subjective,
   304→        subjective_entries=subjective_entries,
   305→        issues_scoped=issues_scoped,
   306→        coverage_open=coverage_open,
   307→    )
   308→    if parts:
   309→        print(colorize(f"\n  Subjective: {', '.join(parts)}.", "cyan"))
   310→        print(colorize("  Run `desloppify show subjective` for details.", "dim"))
   311→

1→"""Post-scan plan workflow nudge.
     2→
     3→Dependency-light module that only imports plan/queue helpers — avoids
     4→the import cycle that previously forced function-level imports in scan.py.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→import logging
    10→
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import plan_aware_queue_breakdown
    12→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
    13→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    14→from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
    15→
    16→_logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    17→
    18→
    19→def print_plan_workflow_nudge(state: dict) -> None:
    20→    """Print a queue-count reminder when plan-start scores exist."""
    21→    try:
    22→        plan = load_plan()
    23→        if not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
    24→            return
    25→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
    26→        queue_total = breakdown.actionable
    27→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    28→        _logger.debug("plan workflow nudge skipped", exc_info=True)
    29→        return
    30→
    31→    if queue_total <= 0:
    32→        return
    33→    print(
    34→        colorize(
    35→            f"  Workflow: {queue_total} queue item{'s' if queue_total != 1 else ''}."
    36→            " Score is frozen until the queue is clear — use `desloppify next` to begin.",
    37→            "dim",
    38→        )
    39→    )

    26→_LEADING_NUM_RE = re.compile(r'^\d+\.\s*')
    27→_HEX8_RE = re.compile(r'^[0-9a-f]{8}$')
    28→
    29→
    30→def _suggest_close_matches(state: dict, plan: dict | None, patterns: list[str]) -> None:
    31→    """Print fuzzy match suggestions for patterns that resolved to zero issues."""
    32→    all_ids: list[str] = list(state.get("issues", {}).keys())
    33→    if plan is not None:
    34→        seen_ids: set[str] = set(all_ids)
    35→        for fid in plan.get("queue_order", []):
    36→            if fid not in seen_ids:
    37→                seen_ids.add(fid)
    38→                all_ids.append(fid)
    39→        for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
    40→            for fid in cluster.get("issue_ids", []):
    41→                if fid not in seen_ids:
    42→                    seen_ids.add(fid)
    43→                    all_ids.append(fid)
    44→
    45→    for pattern in patterns:
    46→        segments = pattern.split("::")
    47→        last_seg = segments[-1]
    48→        suggestions: list[str] = []
    49→
    50→        if _HEX8_RE.match(last_seg):
    51→            suggestions = [fid for fid in all_ids if fid.endswith(f"::{last_seg}") or fid == last_seg]
    52→            if suggestions:
    53→                print(colorize(f"  No match for: {pattern!r}", "yellow"))
    54→                print(colorize("  Did you mean:", "dim"))
    55→                for m in suggestions[:3]:
    56→                    print(colorize(f"    {m}", "dim"))
    57→                print(colorize(f"  Tip: match by hash suffix alone: {last_seg}", "dim"))
    58→            continue
    59→
    60→        # Try last segment and second-to-last as descriptive name slugs
    61→        slug = segments[-2] if len(segments) >= 2 else ""
    62→        for fid in all_ids:
    63→            if f"::{last_seg}::" in fid or fid.endswith(f"::{last_seg}"):
    64→                suggestions.append(fid)
    65→            elif slug and (f"::{slug}::" in fid or fid.endswith(f"::{slug}")):
    66→                suggestions.append(fid)
    67→        if suggestions:
    68→            print(colorize(f"  No match for: {pattern!r}", "yellow"))
    69→            print(colorize("  Did you mean:", "dim"))
    70→            for m in suggestions[:3]:
    71→                print(colorize(f"    {m}", "dim"))
    72→
    73→
    74→def _print_pattern_hints() -> None:
    75→    """Print valid pattern format hints after a no-match error.

    77→    Side-effect only: prints help text to stdout when pattern resolution
    78→    returns zero matches, guiding the user toward valid pattern syntax.

    82→    print(colorize("    review::path::name    (ID prefix)", "dim"))
    83→    print(colorize("    review                (all issues from detector)", "dim"))
    84→    print(colorize("    src/foo.py            (all issues in file)", "dim"))
    85→    print(colorize("    timing_attack         (issue name — last ::segment of ID)", "dim"))
    86→    print(colorize("    review::*naming*      (glob pattern)", "dim"))
    87→    print(colorize("    my-cluster            (cluster name — expands to members)", "dim"))

   108→def _cmd_cluster_add(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   109→    state = command_runtime(args).state

   116→

   121→        _print_pattern_hints()
   122→        _suggest_close_matches(state, plan, patterns)
   123→        return

   127→        for fid in issue_ids:

   138→    member_set = set(issue_ids)
   139→    for other_name, other_cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
   140→        if other_name == cluster_name or other_cluster.get("auto"):
   141→            continue
   142→        other_ids = set(other_cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   143→        if not other_ids:

   145→        overlap = member_set & other_ids
   146→        if len(overlap) > len(other_ids) * 0.5:

   149→                f"({len(overlap)}/{len(other_ids)} = {int(len(overlap)/len(other_ids)*100)}% overlap).",
   150→                "yellow",
   151→            ))
   152→
   153→    append_log_entry(

    55→def _resolve_state_file(path: Path | None) -> Path:

    68→
    69→def _snapshot_file(path: Path) -> str | None:
    70→    if not path.exists():
    71→        return None
    72→    return path.read_text()
    73→
    74→
    75→def _restore_file_snapshot(path: Path, snapshot: str | None) -> None:
    76→    if snapshot is None:
    77→        try:
    78→            path.unlink()
    79→        except FileNotFoundError:
    80→            return
    81→        return
    82→    safe_write_text(path, snapshot)

    85→def _save_plan_state_transactional(
    86→    *,
    87→    plan: dict,
    88→    plan_path: Path | None,
    89→    state_data: dict,
    90→    state_path_value: Path | None,
    91→) -> None:
    92→    """Persist plan+state together; rollback both files on partial write failure."""
    93→    effective_plan_path = _resolve_plan_file(plan_path)
    94→    effective_state_path = _resolve_state_file(state_path_value)
    95→    plan_snapshot = _snapshot_file(effective_plan_path)
    96→    state_snapshot = _snapshot_file(effective_state_path)
    97→
    98→    try:
    99→        state_mod.save_state(state_data, effective_state_path)
   100→        save_plan(plan, effective_plan_path)
   101→    except Exception:
   102→        _restore_file_snapshot(effective_state_path, state_snapshot)
   103→        _restore_file_snapshot(effective_plan_path, plan_snapshot)
   104→        raise

   122→    for fid in issue_ids:

   209→def cmd_plan_skip(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   210→    """Skip issues — unified command for temporary/permanent/false-positive."""
   211→    runtime = command_runtime(args)

   285→def cmd_plan_unskip(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   286→    """Unskip issues — bring back to queue."""
   287→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   288→    state = runtime.state
   289→    if not require_completed_scan(state):

   291→
   292→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])

   294→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   295→    plan_file = _plan_file_for_state(state_file)

   297→    # For unskip we need to match against all statuses (skipped items may be wontfix/fp)
   298→    issue_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan, status_filter="all")
   299→    if not issue_ids:

   302→
   303→    count, need_reopen = unskip_items(plan, issue_ids)
   304→    append_log_entry(

   308→
   309→    # Reopen permanent/false_positive items in state
   310→    reopened: list[str] = []

   322→    else:

   332→def cmd_plan_reopen(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:

   334→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   335→
   336→    raw_state_path = state_path(args)

    69→    try:
    70→        if not has_living_plan():
    71→            return None
    72→        plan = load_plan()
    73→        purged = purge_ids(plan, all_resolved)
    74→        append_log_entry(
    75→            plan,
    76→            "resolve",
    77→            issue_ids=all_resolved,
    78→            actor="user",
    79→            note=getattr(args, "note", None),
    80→            detail={"status": args.status, "attestation": attestation},
    81→        )
    82→        # Commit tracking: add to uncommitted on fix, remove on reopen
    83→        if args.status == "fixed":
    84→            add_uncommitted_issues(plan, all_resolved)
    85→        elif args.status == "open":
    86→            purge_uncommitted_ids(plan, all_resolved)
    87→        save_plan(plan)
    88→        if purged:
    89→            print(colorize(f"  Plan updated: {purged} item(s) removed from queue.", "dim"))
    90→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    91→        _logger.debug("plan update failed after resolve", exc_info=True)
    92→        print(colorize("  Warning: could not update living plan.", "yellow"), file=sys.stderr)
    93→    return plan
    94→
    95→
    96→def cmd_resolve(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    97→    """Resolve issue(s) matching one or more patterns."""
    98→    attestation = getattr(args, "attest", None)
    99→    _validate_resolve_inputs(args, attestation)
   100→    if not _validate_fixed_note(args):
   101→        return
   102→
   103→    state_file = state_path(args)
   104→    state = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   105→
   106→    if _check_queue_order_guard(state, args.patterns, args.status):
   107→        return
   108→
   109→    if args.status == "fixed":
   110→        require_triage_current_or_exit(
   111→            state=state,
   112→            bypass=bool(getattr(args, "force_resolve", False)),
   113→            attest=getattr(args, "attest", "") or "",
   114→        )
   115→
   116→    _enforce_batch_wontfix_confirmation(
   117→        state,
   118→        args,
   119→        attestation=attestation,
   120→        resolve_all_patterns_fn=_resolve_all_patterns,
   121→    )
   122→    prev = _previous_score_snapshot(state)
   123→    prev_subjective_scores = {
   124→        str(dim): (coerce_assessment_score(payload) or 0.0)
   125→        for dim, payload in (state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}).items()
   126→        if isinstance(dim, str)
   127→    }
   128→
   129→    all_resolved = _resolve_all_patterns(state, args, attestation=attestation)
   130→    if not all_resolved:
   131→        status_label = "resolved" if args.status == "open" else "open"
   132→        print(colorize(f"No {status_label} issues matching: {' '.join(args.patterns)}", "yellow"))
   133→        return
   134→
   135→    _save_state_or_exit(state, state_file)
   136→
   137→    plan = _update_living_plan_after_resolve(
   138→        args=args,
   139→        all_resolved=all_resolved,
   140→        attestation=attestation,
   141→    )
   142→
   143→    _print_resolve_summary(status=args.status, all_resolved=all_resolved)
   144→    _print_wontfix_batch_warning(
   145→        state,
   146→        status=args.status,
   147→        resolved_count=len(all_resolved),
   148→    )
   149→    show_score_with_plan_context(state, prev)
   150→    render_commit_guidance(state, plan, all_resolved, args.status)

    21→def _print_resolve_summary(*, status: str, all_resolved: list[str]) -> None:
    22→    verb = "Reopened" if status == "open" else "Resolved"
    23→    print(colorize(f"\n{verb} {len(all_resolved)} issue(s) as {status}:", "green"))
    24→    for fid in all_resolved[:20]:
    25→        print(f"  {fid}")
    26→    if len(all_resolved) > 20:
    27→        print(f"  ... and {len(all_resolved) - 20} more")
    28→
    29→
    30→def _print_wontfix_batch_warning(
    31→    state: dict,
    32→    *,
    33→    status: str,
    34→    resolved_count: int,
    35→) -> None:
    36→    if status != "wontfix" or resolved_count <= 10:
    37→        return
    38→    wontfix_count = sum(
    39→        1 for issue in state["issues"].values() if issue["status"] == "wontfix"
    40→    )
    41→    actionable = sum(
    42→        1
    43→        for issue in state["issues"].values()
    44→        if issue["status"]
    45→        in ("open", "wontfix", "fixed", "auto_resolved", "false_positive")
    46→    )
    47→    wontfix_pct = round(wontfix_count / actionable * 100) if actionable else 0
    48→    print(
    49→        colorize(
    50→            f"\n  ⚠ Wontfix debt is now {wontfix_count} issues ({wontfix_pct}% of actionable).",
    51→            "yellow",
    52→        )
    53→    )
    54→    print(
    55→        colorize(
    56→            '    The strict score reflects this. Run `desloppify show "*" --status wontfix` to review.',
    57→            "dim",
    58→        )
    59→    )
    60→
    61→
    62→def _delta_suffix(delta: float) -> str:
    63→    if abs(delta) < 0.05:
    64→        return ""
    65→    return f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})"
    66→
    67→
    68→def _print_score_movement(
    69→    *,
    70→    status: str,
    71→    prev_overall: float | None,
    72→    prev_objective: float | None,
    73→    prev_strict: float | None,
    74→    prev_verified: float | None,
    75→    state: dict,
    76→    has_review_issues: bool = False,
    77→    target_strict: float | None = None,
    78→) -> None:
    79→    new = score_snapshot_or_warn(state)
    80→    if new is None:
    81→        return
    82→
    83→    overall_delta = new.overall - (prev_overall or 0)
    84→    objective_delta = new.objective - (prev_objective or 0)
    85→    strict_delta = new.strict - (prev_strict or 0)
    86→    verified_delta = new.verified - (prev_verified or 0)
    87→    print(
    88→        f"\n  Scores: overall {new.overall:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(overall_delta)}"
    89→        + colorize(
    90→            f"  objective {new.objective:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(objective_delta)}",
    91→            "dim",
    92→        )
    93→        + colorize(f"  strict {new.strict:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(strict_delta)}", "dim")
    94→        + colorize(
    95→            f"  verified {new.verified:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(verified_delta)}", "dim"
    96→        )
    97→    )
    98→    if target_strict is not None:
    99→        print_strict_target_nudge(new.strict, target_strict, show_next=False)
   100→    print_strict_gap_note(status, overall=new.overall, strict=new.strict)
   101→    print_post_resolve_guidance(
   102→        status=status,
   103→        has_review_issues=has_review_issues,
   104→        overall_delta=overall_delta,
   105→    )
   106→
   107→
   108→def _print_subjective_reset_hint(
   109→    *,
   110→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   111→    state: dict,
   112→    all_resolved: list[str],
   113→    prev_subjective_scores: dict[str, float],
   114→) -> None:
   115→    has_review = any(
   116→        state["issues"].get(fid, {}).get("detector") == "review"
   117→        for fid in all_resolved
   118→    )
   119→    if not has_review or not state.get("subjective_assessments"):
   120→        return
   121→
   122→    stale_dims = sorted(
   123→        dim
   124→        for dim in {
   125→            str(
   126→                state["issues"].get(fid, {}).get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")
   127→            ).strip()
   128→            for fid in all_resolved
   129→            if state["issues"].get(fid, {}).get("detector") == "review"
   130→        }
   131→        if dim and dim in (state.get("subjective_assessments") or {})
   132→    )
   133→    if not stale_dims:
   134→        return
   135→
   136→    shown = ", ".join(stale_dims[:3])
   137→    if len(stale_dims) > 3:
   138→        shown = f"{shown}, +{len(stale_dims) - 3} more"
   139→    print(
   140→        colorize(
   141→            f"  Subjective scores unchanged — re-run review for updated scores: {shown}",
   142→            "yellow",
   143→        )
   144→    )
   145→    print(
   146→        colorize(
   147→            "  Next subjective step: "
   148→            + (
   149→                "`desloppify review --prepare --dimensions "
   150→                f"{','.join(stale_dims)} --force-review-rerun`"

1→"""Output helpers for the `next` command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
     7→from typing import Any
     8→
     9→from desloppify.base.output.fallbacks import print_write_error
    10→
    11→_CLUSTER_MEMBER_SAMPLE_LIMIT = 25
    12→
    13→
    14→def _serialize_cluster_member(member: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    15→    """Serialize a cluster member without nested plan metadata."""
    16→    return {
    17→        "id": member.get("id"),
    18→        "confidence": member.get("confidence"),
    19→        "detector": member.get("detector"),
    20→        "file": member.get("file"),
    21→        "summary": member.get("summary"),
    22→        "status": member.get("status"),
    23→        "primary_command": member.get("primary_command"),
    24→    }
    25→
    26→
    27→def serialize_item(item: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    28→    """Build a serializable output dict from a queue item."""
    29→    # Cluster meta-items get their own serialization
    30→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    31→        members_raw = item.get("members", [])
    32→        serialized_members = [
    33→            _serialize_cluster_member(member)
    34→            for member in members_raw[:_CLUSTER_MEMBER_SAMPLE_LIMIT]
    35→        ]
    36→        member_count = int(item.get("member_count", len(members_raw)))
    37→        serialized_cluster: dict[str, Any] = {
    38→            "id": item.get("id"),
    39→            "kind": "cluster",
    40→            "action_type": item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"),
    41→            "summary": item.get("summary"),
    42→            "member_count": member_count,
    43→            "members": serialized_members,
    44→            "cluster_name": item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id")),
    45→            "cluster_auto": item.get("cluster_auto", True),
    46→            "detector": item.get("detector"),
    47→        }
    48→        if member_count > len(serialized_members):
    49→            serialized_cluster["members_truncated"] = True
    50→            serialized_cluster["members_sample_limit"] = _CLUSTER_MEMBER_SAMPLE_LIMIT
    51→        serialized_cluster["primary_command"] = item.get("primary_command")
    52→        return serialized_cluster
    53→
    54→    serialized: dict[str, Any] = {
    55→        "id": item.get("id"),
    56→        "kind": item.get("kind", "issue"),
    57→        "confidence": item.get("confidence"),
    58→        "detector": item.get("detector"),
    59→        "file": item.get("file"),
    60→        "summary": item.get("summary"),
    61→        "detail": item.get("detail", {}),
    62→        "status": item.get("status"),
    63→    }
    64→    serialized["primary_command"] = item.get("primary_command")
    65→
    66→    # Workflow dependency state
    67→    if item.get("blocked_by"):
    68→        serialized["blocked_by"] = item["blocked_by"]
    69→    if item.get("is_blocked"):
    70→        serialized["is_blocked"] = True
    71→
    72→    explain = item.get("explain")
    73→    if explain is not None:
    74→        serialized["explain"] = explain
    75→
    76→    # Plan metadata
    77→    if item.get("queue_position"):
    78→        serialized["queue_position"] = item["queue_position"]
    79→    if item.get("plan_description"):
    80→        serialized["plan_description"] = item["plan_description"]
    81→    if item.get("plan_note"):
    82→        serialized["plan_note"] = item["plan_note"]
    83→    if item.get("plan_cluster"):
    84→        serialized["plan_cluster"] = item["plan_cluster"]
    85→    if item.get("plan_skipped"):
    86→        serialized["plan_skipped"] = True
    87→        serialized["plan_skip_kind"] = item.get("plan_skip_kind", "temporary")
    88→        if item.get("plan_skip_reason"):
    89→            serialized["plan_skip_reason"] = item["plan_skip_reason"]
    90→
    91→    return serialized
    92→
    93→
    94→def build_query_payload(

    97→    *,
    98→    command: str,
    99→    narrative: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
   100→    plan: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,

    43→def _load_scores(state: StateModel) -> state_mod.ScoreSnapshot:

    46→
    47→
    48→def _print_score_lines(
    49→    *,
    50→    overall_score: float | None,
    51→    objective_score: float | None,
    52→    strict_score: float | None,
    53→    verified_strict_score: float | None,
    54→) -> None:
    55→    lines: list[str] = []
    56→    if overall_score is not None:
    57→        lines.append(f"Overall score:   {overall_score:.1f}/100")
    58→    if objective_score is not None:
    59→        lines.append(f"Objective score: {objective_score:.1f}/100")
    60→    if strict_score is not None:
    61→        lines.append(f"Strict score:    {strict_score:.1f}/100")
    62→    if verified_strict_score is not None:
    63→        lines.append(f"Verified score:  {verified_strict_score:.1f}/100")
    64→    if lines:
    65→        print("\n".join(lines))
    66→    # Score legend — always shown in LLM block so agents understand the scoring model
    67→    print("Score guide:")
    68→    print("  overall  = 40% mechanical + 60% subjective (lenient — ignores wontfix)")
    69→    print("  objective = mechanical detectors only (no subjective review)")
    70→    print("  strict   = like overall, but wontfix counts against you  <-- your north star")
    71→    print("  verified = strict, but only credits scan-verified fixes")

    73→
    74→
    75→def _split_dimension_scores(
    76→    state: StateModel,
    77→    dim_scores: dict[str, Any],
    78→) -> tuple[list[tuple[str, dict[str, Any]]], list[tuple[str, dict[str, Any]]]]:
    79→    # Build dimension table from canonical scorecard projection.
    80→    rows = scorecard_projection_mod.scorecard_dimension_rows(
    81→        state, dim_scores=dim_scores
    82→    )
    83→    subjective_name_set = {name.lower() for name in DISPLAY_NAMES.values()}
    84→    subjective_name_set.update({"elegance", "elegance (combined)"})
    85→
    86→    mechanical = [
    87→        (name, data)
    88→        for name, data in rows
    89→        if (
    90→            "subjective_assessment" not in data.get("detectors", {})
    91→            and str(name).strip().lower() not in subjective_name_set
    92→        )
    93→    ]
    94→    subjective = [
    95→        (name, data)
    96→        for name, data in rows
    97→        if (
    98→            "subjective_assessment" in data.get("detectors", {})
    99→            or str(name).strip().lower() in subjective_name_set
   100→        )
   101→    ]
   102→    return mechanical, subjective
   103→
   104→
   105→def _print_dimension_table(state: StateModel, dim_scores: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   106→    mechanical, subjective = _split_dimension_scores(state, dim_scores)
   107→    if not (mechanical or subjective):
   108→        return
   109→
   110→    print("| Dimension | Health | Strict | Issues | Tier | Action |")
   111→    print("|-----------|--------|--------|--------|------|--------|")
   112→    for name, data in sorted(mechanical, key=lambda item: item[0]):
   113→        score = data.get("score", 100)
   114→        strict = data.get("strict", score)
   115→        issues = data.get("failing", 0)
   116→        tier = data.get("tier", "")
   117→        action = registry_mod.dimension_action_type(name)
   118→        print(
   119→            f"| {name} | {score:.1f}% | {strict:.1f}% | {issues} | T{tier} | {action} |"
   120→        )
   121→    if subjective:
   122→        print("| **Subjective Dimensions** | | | | | |")
   123→        for name, data in sorted(subjective, key=lambda item: item[0]):
   124→            score = data.get("score", 100)
   125→            strict = data.get("strict", score)
   126→            issues = data.get("failing", 0)
   127→            tier = data.get("tier", "")
   128→            print(
   129→                f"| {name} | {score:.1f}% | {strict:.1f}% | {issues} | T{tier} | review |"
   130→            )
   131→    print()
   132→
   133→
   134→def _print_drag_summary(dim_scores: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   135→    """Print the biggest score-drag dimensions so agents know where to focus."""
   136→    if not dim_scores:
   137→        return
   138→    try:
   139→        breakdown = compute_health_breakdown(dim_scores)
   140→        entries = breakdown.get("entries", [])
   141→        drags = sorted(
   142→            [e for e in entries if isinstance(e, dict) and float(e.get("overall_drag", 0) or 0) > 0.01],
   143→            key=lambda e: -float(e.get("overall_drag", 0) or 0),
   144→        )
   145→        if drags:
   146→            print("Biggest score drags (fixing these dimensions has the most impact):")

    28→def write_show_output_file(output_file: str, payload: dict, surfaced_count: int) -> bool:
    29→    """Write serialized show payload to file."""
    30→    try:
    31→        safe_write_text(output_file, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
    32→        print(colorize(f"Wrote {surfaced_count} issues to {output_file}", "green"))
    33→    except OSError as exc:
    34→        payload["output_error"] = str(exc)
    35→        print_write_error(output_file, exc, label="show output")
    36→        return False
    37→    return True
    38→
    39→
    40→def group_matches_by_file(matches: list[dict]) -> list[tuple[str, list]]:
    41→    """Group issues by file and sort by descending count."""
    42→    by_file: dict[str, list] = defaultdict(list)
    43→    for issue in matches:
    44→        by_file[issue["file"]].append(issue)
    45→    return sorted(by_file.items(), key=lambda item: -len(item[1]))
    46→
    47→
    48→def _print_single_issue(issue: dict, *, show_code: bool) -> None:
    49→    """Render a single issue to terminal."""
    50→    normalized_status = canonical_issue_status(issue.get("status"))
    51→    status_icon = {
    52→        "open": "○",
    53→        "fixed": "✓",
    54→        "wontfix": "—",
    55→        "false_positive": "✗",
    56→        "auto_resolved": "◌",
    57→    }.get(normalized_status, "?")
    58→    zone = issue.get("zone", "production")
    59→    zone_tag = colorize(f" [{zone}]", "dim") if zone != "production" else ""
    60→    print(
    61→        f"    {status_icon} T{issue['tier']} [{issue['confidence']}] {issue['summary']}{zone_tag}"
    62→    )
    63→
    64→    detail_parts = format_detail(issue.get("detail", {}))
    65→    if detail_parts:
    66→        print(colorize(f"      {' · '.join(detail_parts)}", "dim"))
    67→    if show_code:
    68→        detail = issue.get("detail", {})
    69→        target_line = (
    70→            detail.get("line") or (detail.get("lines", [None]) or [None])[0]
    71→        )
    72→        if target_line and issue["file"] not in (".", ""):
    73→            snippet = read_code_snippet(issue["file"], target_line)
    74→            if snippet:
    75→                print(snippet)
    76→    if issue.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2:
    77→        print(

    80→                "red",
    81→            )
    82→        )
    83→    if issue.get("note"):
    84→        print(colorize(f"      note: {issue['note']}", "dim"))

    86→
    87→

   114→        )

   144→        )
   145→
   146→        for issue in issues:

   148→        print()
   149→
   150→    if remaining_issues:
